Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(FunctionType,JSDocInfo,Node)

Documentation:
/**
     * If a variable is assigned a function literal in the global scope,
     * make that a declared type (even if there's no doc info).
     * There's only one exception to this rule:
     * if the return type is inferred, and we're in a local
     * scope, we should assume the whole function is inferred.
     */
Method code:
780 |     private boolean shouldUseFunctionLiteralType(
781 |         FunctionType type, JSDocInfo info, Node lValue) {
782 |       if (info != null) {
783 |         return true;
784 |       }
785 |       if (lValue != null &&
786 |           NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(lValue, lValue.getParent())) {
787 |         return false;
788 |       }
789 |       return scope.isGlobal() || !type.isReturnTypeInferred();
790 |     }

===== M0022 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.ensureTyped(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1835-1859
Documentation:
/**
   * Enforces type casts, and ensures the node is typed.
   *
   * A cast in the way that we use it in JSDoc annotations never
   * alters the generated code and therefore never can induce any runtime
   * operation. What this means is that a 'cast' is really just a compile
   * time constraint on the underlying value. In the future, we may add
   * support for run-time casts for compiled tests.
   *
   * To ensure some shred of sanity, we enforce the notion that the
   * type you are casting to may only meaningfully be a narrower type
   * than the underlying declared type. We also invalidate optimizations
   * on bad type casts.
   *
   * @param t The traversal object needed to report errors.
   * @param n The node getting a type assigned to it.
   * @param type The type to be assigned.
   */
Method code:
1835 |   private void ensureTyped(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type) {
1836 |     // Make sure FUNCTION nodes always get function type.
1837 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
1838 |             type.isFunctionType() ||
1839 |             type.isUnknownType());
1840 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1841 |     if (info != null) {
1842 |       if (info.hasType()) {
1843 |         JSType infoType = info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry);
1844 |         validator.expectCanCast(t, n, infoType, type);
1845 |         type = infoType;
1846 |       }
1847 | 
1848 |       if (info.isImplicitCast() && !inExterns) {
1849 |         String propName = n.isGetProp() ?
1850 |             n.getLastChild().getString() : "(missing)";
1851 |         compiler.report(
1852 |             t.makeError(n, ILLEGAL_IMPLICIT_CAST, propName));
1853 |       }
1854 |     }
1855 | 
1856 |     if (n.getJSType() == null) {
1857 |       n.setJSType(type);
1858 |     }
1859 |   }

===== M0023 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java
Original range: 489-509
Documentation:
/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
489 |   @Override
490 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
491 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
492 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
493 |     if (unknown) {
494 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
495 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
496 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
497 |         unknown = false;
498 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
499 |           if (interfaceType.isUnknownType()) {
500 |             unknown = true;
501 |             break;
502 |           }
503 |         }
504 |       } else {
505 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
506 |       }
507 |     }
508 |     return unknown;
509 |   }

===== M0025 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.createFunctionTypeFromNodes(Node,String,JSDocInfo,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 814-915
Documentation:
/**
     * Creates a new function type, based on the given nodes.
     *
     * This handles two cases that are semantically very different, but
     * are not mutually exclusive:
     * - A function literal that needs a type attached to it.
     * - An assignment expression with function-type info in the jsdoc.
     *
     * All parameters are optional, and we will do the best we can to create
     * a function type.
     *
     * This function will always create a function type, so only call it if
     * you're sure that's what you want.
     *
     * @param rValue The function node.
     * @param name the function's name
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} attached to the function definition
     * @param lvalueNode The node where this function is being
     *     assigned. For example, {@code A.prototype.foo = ...} would be used to
     *     determine that this function is a method of A.prototype. May be
     *     null to indicate that this is not being assigned to a qualified name.
     */
Method code:
814 |     private FunctionType createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
815 |         @Nullable Node rValue,
816 |         @Nullable String name,
817 |         @Nullable JSDocInfo info,
818 |         @Nullable Node lvalueNode) {
819 | 
820 |       FunctionType functionType = null;
821 | 
822 |       // Global ctor aliases should be registered with the type registry.
823 |       if (rValue != null && rValue.isQualifiedName() && scope.isGlobal()) {
824 |         Var var = scope.getVar(rValue.getQualifiedName());
825 |         if (var != null && var.getType() != null &&
826 |             var.getType().isFunctionType()) {
827 |           FunctionType aliasedType  = var.getType().toMaybeFunctionType();
828 |           if ((aliasedType.isConstructor() || aliasedType.isInterface()) &&
829 |               !aliasedType.isNativeObjectType()) {
830 |             functionType = aliasedType;
831 | 
832 |             if (name != null && scope.isGlobal()) {
833 |               typeRegistry.declareType(name, functionType.getInstanceType());
834 |             }
835 |           }
836 |         }
837 |       }
838 | 
839 |       if (functionType == null) {
840 |         Node errorRoot = rValue == null ? lvalueNode : rValue;
841 |         boolean isFnLiteral =
842 |             rValue != null && rValue.isFunction();
843 |         Node fnRoot = isFnLiteral ? rValue : null;
844 |         Node parametersNode = isFnLiteral ?
845 |             rValue.getFirstChild().getNext() : null;
846 |         Node fnBlock = isFnLiteral ? parametersNode.getNext() : null;
847 | 
848 |         if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
849 |           JSType type = info.getType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
850 | 
851 |           // Known to be not null since we have the FUNCTION token there.
852 |           type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
853 |           if (type.isFunctionType()) {
854 |             functionType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
855 |             functionType.setJSDocInfo(info);
856 |           }
857 |         }
858 | 
859 |         if (functionType == null) {
860 |           // Find the type of any overridden function.
861 |           Node ownerNode = NodeUtil.getBestLValueOwner(lvalueNode);
862 |           String ownerName = NodeUtil.getBestLValueName(ownerNode);
863 |           Var ownerVar = null;
864 |           String propName = null;
865 |           ObjectType ownerType = null;
866 |           if (ownerName != null) {
867 |             ownerVar = scope.getVar(ownerName);
868 |             if (ownerVar != null) {
869 |               ownerType = ObjectType.cast(ownerVar.getType());
870 |             }
871 |             if (name != null) {
872 |               propName = name.substring(ownerName.length() + 1);
873 |             }
874 |           }
875 | 
876 |           FunctionType overriddenPropType = null;
877 |           if (ownerType != null && propName != null) {
878 |             overriddenPropType =
879 |                 findOverriddenFunction(ownerType, propName);
880 |           }
881 | 
882 |           FunctionTypeBuilder builder =
883 |               new FunctionTypeBuilder(name, compiler, errorRoot, sourceName,
884 |                   scope)
885 |               .setContents(getFunctionAnalysisResults(fnRoot))
886 |               .inferFromOverriddenFunction(overriddenPropType, parametersNode)
887 |               .inferTemplateTypeName(info)
888 |               .inferReturnType(info)
889 |               .inferInheritance(info);
890 | 
891 | 
892 |           // Infer the context type.
893 |           boolean searchedForThisType = false;
894 |           if (ownerType != null && ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType()) {
895 |             builder.inferThisType(
896 |                 info, ownerType.getOwnerFunction().getInstanceType());
897 |             searchedForThisType = true;
898 |           } else if (ownerNode != null && ownerNode.isThis()) {
899 |             builder.inferThisType(info, ownerNode.getJSType());
900 |             searchedForThisType = true;
901 |           }
902 | 
903 |           if (!searchedForThisType) {
904 |             builder.inferThisType(info);
905 |           }
906 | 
907 |           functionType = builder
908 |               .inferParameterTypes(parametersNode, info)
909 |               .buildAndRegister();
910 |         }
911 |       }
912 | 
913 |       // all done
914 |       return functionType;
915 |     }

===== M0028 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(StaticSourceFile,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 104-148
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
104 |   public static Node parse(StaticSourceFile sourceFile,
105 |                            String sourceString,
106 |                            Config config,
107 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
108 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
109 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
110 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
111 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
112 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
113 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
114 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
115 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
116 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
117 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
118 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
119 | 
120 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
121 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
122 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
123 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
124 |     }
125 | 
126 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
127 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
128 |     }
129 |     compilerEnv.setIdeMode(config.isIdeMode);
130 | 
131 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
132 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
133 |     try {
134 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceFile.getName(), 1);
135 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
136 |       logger.info(
137 |           "Error parsing " + sourceFile.getName() + ": " + e.getMessage());
138 |     } finally {
139 |       Context.exit();
140 |     }
141 |     Node root = null;
142 |     if (astRoot != null) {
143 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
144 |           astRoot, sourceFile, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
145 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
146 |     }
147 |     return root;
148 |   }

===== M0031 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineName(Node,Node,Node,JSDocInfo)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 760-771
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a variable based on the {@link Token#NAME} node passed.
     * @param name The {@link Token#NAME} node.
     * @param var The parent of the {@code name} node, which must be a
     *     {@link Token#VAR} node.
     * @param parent {@code var}'s parent.
     * @param info the {@link JSDocInfo} information relating to this
     *     {@code name} node.
     */
Method code:
760 |     private void defineName(Node name, Node var, Node parent, JSDocInfo info) {
761 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
762 | 
763 |       // variable's type
764 |       JSType type = getDeclaredType(sourceName, info, name, value);
765 |       if (type == null) {
766 |         // The variable's type will be inferred.
767 |         type = name.isFromExterns() ?
768 |             getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : null;

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 617-629
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
617 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
618 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
619 |     if (typeSet == null) {
620 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this, PROPERTY_CHECKING_UNION_SIZE);
621 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
622 |     }
623 | 
624 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
625 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
626 | 
627 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
628 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
629 |   }

===== M0055 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitCall(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1545-1597
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a CALL node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1545 |   private void visitCall(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1546 |     Node child = n.getFirstChild();
1547 |     JSType childType = getJSType(child).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1548 | 
1549 |     if (!childType.canBeCalled()) {
1550 |       report(t, n, NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1551 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1552 |       return;
1553 |     }
1554 | 
1555 |     // A couple of types can be called as if they were functions.
1556 |     // If it is a function type, then validate parameters.
1557 |     if (childType.isFunctionType()) {
1558 |       FunctionType functionType = childType.toMaybeFunctionType();
1559 | 
1560 |       boolean isExtern = false;
1561 |       JSDocInfo functionJSDocInfo = functionType.getJSDocInfo();
1562 |       if( functionJSDocInfo != null  &&
1563 |           functionJSDocInfo.getAssociatedNode() != null) {
1564 |         isExtern = functionJSDocInfo.getAssociatedNode().isFromExterns();
1565 |       }
1566 | 
1567 |       // Non-native constructors should not be called directly
1568 |       // unless they specify a return type and are defined
1569 |       // in an extern.
1570 |       if (functionType.isConstructor() &&
1571 |           !functionType.isNativeObjectType() &&
1572 |           (functionType.getReturnType().isUnknownType() ||
1573 |            functionType.getReturnType().isVoidType() ||
1574 |            !isExtern)) {
1575 |         report(t, n, CONSTRUCTOR_NOT_CALLABLE, childType.toString());
1576 |       }
1577 | 
1578 |       // Functions with explcit 'this' types must be called in a GETPROP
1579 |       // or GETELEM.
1580 |       if (functionType.isOrdinaryFunction() &&
1581 |           !functionType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType() &&
1582 |           !functionType.getTypeOfThis().isNativeObjectType() &&
1583 |           !(child.isGetElem() ||
1584 |             child.isGetProp())) {
1585 |         report(t, n, EXPECTED_THIS_TYPE, functionType.toString());
1586 |       }
1587 | 
1588 |       visitParameterList(t, n, functionType);
1589 |       ensureTyped(t, n, functionType.getReturnType());
1590 |     } else {
1591 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1592 |     }
1593 | 
1594 |     // TODO: Add something to check for calls of RegExp objects, which is not
1595 |     // supported by IE.  Either say something about the return type or warn
1596 |     // about the non-portability of the call or both.
1597 |   }

===== M0056 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType,String,JSDocInfo,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1019-1120
Documentation:
/**
   * Given a constructor type and a property name, check that the property has
   * the JSDoc annotation @override iff the property is declared on a
   * superclass. Several checks regarding inheritance correctness are also
   * performed.
   */
Method code:
1019 |   private void checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
1020 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, FunctionType ctorType, String propertyName,
1021 |       JSDocInfo info, JSType propertyType) {
1022 |     // If the supertype doesn't resolve correctly, we've warned about this
1023 |     // already.
1024 |     if (hasUnknownOrEmptySupertype(ctorType)) {
1025 |       return;
1026 |     }
1027 | 
1028 |     FunctionType superClass = ctorType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1029 |     boolean superClassHasProperty = superClass != null &&
1030 |         superClass.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1031 |     // For interface
1032 |     boolean superInterfacesHasProperty = false;
1033 |     if (ctorType.isInterface()) {
1034 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1035 |         superInterfacesHasProperty =
1036 |           superInterfacesHasProperty || interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName);
1037 |       }
1038 |     }
1039 |     boolean declaredOverride = info != null && info.isOverride();
1040 | 
1041 |     boolean foundInterfaceProperty = false;
1042 |     if (ctorType.isConstructor()) {
1043 |       for (JSType implementedInterface :
1044 |           ctorType.getAllImplementedInterfaces()) {
1045 |         if (implementedInterface.isUnknownType() ||
1046 |             implementedInterface.isEmptyType()) {
1047 |           continue;
1048 |         }
1049 |         FunctionType interfaceType =
1050 |             implementedInterface.toObjectType().getConstructor();
1051 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(interfaceType);
1052 |         boolean interfaceHasProperty =
1053 |             interfaceType.getPrototype().hasProperty(propertyName);
1054 |         foundInterfaceProperty = foundInterfaceProperty || interfaceHasProperty;
1055 |         if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && !declaredOverride &&
1056 |             interfaceHasProperty) {
1057 |           // @override not present, but the property does override an interface
1058 |           // property
1059 |           compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1060 |               HIDDEN_INTERFACE_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1061 |               interfaceType.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName).toString()));
1062 |         }
1063 |       }
1064 |     }
1065 | 
1066 |     if (!declaredOverride && !superClassHasProperty
1067 |         && !superInterfacesHasProperty) {
1068 |       // nothing to do here, it's just a plain new property
1069 |       return;
1070 |     }
1071 | 
1072 |     JSType topInstanceType = superClassHasProperty ?
1073 |         superClass.getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName) : null;
1074 |     if (reportMissingOverride.isOn() && ctorType.isConstructor() &&
1075 |         !declaredOverride && superClassHasProperty) {
1076 |       // @override not present, but the property does override a superclass
1077 |       // property
1078 |       compiler.report(t.makeError(n, reportMissingOverride,
1079 |           HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY, propertyName,
1080 |           topInstanceType.toString()));
1081 |     }
1082 |     if (!declaredOverride) {
1083 |       // there's no @override to check
1084 |       return;
1085 |     }
1086 |     // @override is present and we have to check that it is ok
1087 |     if (superClassHasProperty) {
1088 |       // there is a superclass implementation
1089 |       JSType superClassPropType =
1090 |           superClass.getPrototype().getPropertyType(propertyName);
1091 |       if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superClassPropType)) {
1092 |         compiler.report(
1093 |             t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1094 |                 propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1095 |                 superClassPropType.toString(), propertyType.toString()));
1096 |       }
1097 |     } else if (superInterfacesHasProperty) {
1098 |       // there is an super interface property
1099 |       for (ObjectType interfaceType : ctorType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1100 |         if (interfaceType.hasProperty(propertyName)) {
1101 |           JSType superPropertyType =
1102 |             interfaceType.getPropertyType(propertyName);
1103 |           if (!propertyType.canAssignTo(superPropertyType)) {
1104 |             topInstanceType = interfaceType.getConstructor().
1105 |                 getTopMostDefiningType(propertyName);
1106 |             compiler.report(
1107 |                 t.makeError(n, HIDDEN_SUPERCLASS_PROPERTY_MISMATCH,
1108 |                     propertyName, topInstanceType.toString(),
1109 |                     superPropertyType.toString(),
1110 |                     propertyType.toString()));
1111 |           }
1112 |         }
1113 |       }
1114 |     } else if (!foundInterfaceProperty) {
1115 |       // there is no superclass nor interface implementation
1116 |       compiler.report(
1117 |           t.makeError(n, UNKNOWN_OVERRIDE,
1118 |               propertyName, ctorType.getInstanceType().toString()));
1119 |     }
1120 |   }

===== M0057 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0063 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 871-913
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
871 |   @Override
872 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
873 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
874 |       return true;
875 |     }
876 | 
877 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
878 |       FunctionType other = that.toMaybeFunctionType();
879 |       if (other.isInterface()) {
880 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
881 |         return true;
882 |       }
883 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
884 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
885 |         return false;
886 |       }
887 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
888 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
889 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
890 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
891 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
892 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
893 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
894 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
895 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
896 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
897 | 
898 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
899 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
900 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
901 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
902 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
903 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
904 | 
905 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
906 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
907 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
908 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
909 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
910 |     }
911 | 
912 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
913 |   }

===== M0079 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 148-190
Documentation:

Method code:
148 |   @Override
149 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
150 |     this.root = root;
151 |     astPositionCounter = 0;
152 |     astPosition = Maps.newHashMap();
153 |     nodePriorities = Maps.newHashMap();
154 |     cfg = new AstControlFlowGraph(computeFallThrough(root), nodePriorities,
155 |                                   edgeAnnotations);
156 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, this);
157 |     astPosition.put(null, ++astPositionCounter); // the implicit return is last.
158 | 
159 |     // Now, generate the priority of nodes by doing a depth-first
160 |     // search on the CFG.
161 |     priorityCounter = 0;
162 |     DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry = cfg.getEntry();
163 |     prioritizeFromEntryNode(entry);
164 | 
165 |     if (shouldTraverseFunctions) {
166 |       // If we're traversing inner functions, we need to rank the
167 |       // priority of them too.
168 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
169 |         Node value = candidate.getValue();
170 |         if (value != null && value.isFunction()) {
171 |           Preconditions.checkState(
172 |               !nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate) || candidate == entry);
173 |           prioritizeFromEntryNode(candidate);
174 |         }
175 |       }
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     // At this point, all reachable nodes have been given a priority, but
179 |     // unreachable nodes have not been given a priority. Put them last.
180 |     // Presumably, it doesn't really matter what priority they get, since
181 |     // this shouldn't happen in real code.
182 |     for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : cfg.getDirectedGraphNodes()) {
183 |       if (!nodePriorities.containsKey(candidate)) {
184 |         nodePriorities.put(candidate, ++priorityCounter);
185 |       }
186 |     }
187 | 
188 |     // Again, the implicit return node is always last.
189 |     nodePriorities.put(cfg.getImplicitReturn(), ++priorityCounter);
190 |   }

===== M0082 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFollowNode(Node,Node,ControlFlowAnalysis)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 682-779
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the follow() node of a given node and its parent. There is a side
   * effect when calling this function. If this function computed an edge that
   * exists a FINALLY, it'll attempt to connect the fromNode to the outer
   * FINALLY according to the finallyMap.
   *
   * @param fromNode The original source node since {@code node} is changed
   *        during recursion.
   * @param node The node that follow() should compute.
   */
Method code:
682 |   private static Node computeFollowNode(
683 |       Node fromNode, Node node, ControlFlowAnalysis cfa) {
684 |     /*
685 |      * This is the case where:
686 |      *
687 |      * 1. Parent is null implies that we are transferring control to the end of
688 |      * the script.
689 |      *
690 |      * 2. Parent is a function implies that we are transferring control back to
691 |      * the caller of the function.
692 |      *
693 |      * 3. If the node is a return statement, we should also transfer control
694 |      * back to the caller of the function.
695 |      *
696 |      * 4. If the node is root then we have reached the end of what we have been
697 |      * asked to traverse.
698 |      *
699 |      * In all cases we should transfer control to a "symbolic return" node.
700 |      * This will make life easier for DFAs.
701 |      */
702 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
703 |     if (parent == null || parent.isFunction() ||
704 |         (cfa != null && node == cfa.root)) {
705 |       return null;
706 |     }
707 | 
708 |     // If we are just before a IF/WHILE/DO/FOR:
709 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
710 |       // The follow() of any of the path from IF would be what follows IF.
711 |       case Token.IF:
712 |         return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
713 |       case Token.CASE:
714 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
715 |         // After the body of a CASE, the control goes to the body of the next
716 |         // case, without having to go to the case condition.
717 |         if (parent.getNext() != null) {
718 |           if (parent.getNext().isCase()) {
719 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild().getNext();
720 |           } else if (parent.getNext().isDefaultCase()) {
721 |             return parent.getNext().getFirstChild();
722 |           } else {
723 |             Preconditions.checkState(false, "Not reachable");
724 |           }
725 |         } else {
726 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
727 |         }
728 |         break;
729 |       case Token.FOR:
730 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
731 |           return parent;
732 |         } else {
733 |           return parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
734 |         }
735 |       case Token.WHILE:
736 |       case Token.DO:
737 |         return parent;
738 |       case Token.TRY:
739 |         // If we are coming out of the TRY block...
740 |         if (parent.getFirstChild() == node) {
741 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
742 |             return computeFallThrough(parent.getLastChild());
743 |           } else { // and have no FINALLY.
744 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
745 |           }
746 |         // CATCH block.
747 |         } else if (NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node){
748 |           if (NodeUtil.hasFinally(parent)) { // and have FINALLY block.
749 |             return computeFallThrough(node.getNext());
750 |           } else {
751 |             return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
752 |           }
753 |         // If we are coming out of the FINALLY block...
754 |         } else if (parent.getLastChild() == node){
755 |           if (cfa != null) {
756 |             for (Node finallyNode : cfa.finallyMap.get(parent)) {
757 |               cfa.createEdge(fromNode, Branch.UNCOND, finallyNode);
758 |             }
759 |           }
760 |           return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
761 |         }
762 |     }
763 | 
764 |     // Now that we are done with the special cases follow should be its
765 |     // immediate sibling, unless its sibling is a function
766 |     Node nextSibling = node.getNext();
767 | 
768 |     // Skip function declarations because control doesn't get pass into it.
769 |     while (nextSibling != null && nextSibling.isFunction()) {
770 |       nextSibling = nextSibling.getNext();
771 |     }
772 | 
773 |     if (nextSibling != null) {
774 |       return computeFallThrough(nextSibling);
775 |     } else {
776 |       // If there are no more siblings, control is transfered up the AST.
777 |       return computeFollowNode(fromNode, parent, cfa);
778 |     }
779 |   }

===== M0091 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.branchedFlowThrough(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 135-247
Documentation:

Method code:
135 |   @Override
136 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
137 |   List<FlowScope> branchedFlowThrough(Node source, FlowScope input) {
138 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): Right now, we just treat ON_EX edges like UNCOND
139 |     // edges. If we wanted to be perfect, we'd actually JOIN all the out
140 |     // lattices of this flow with the in lattice, and then make that the out
141 |     // lattice for the ON_EX edge. But it's probably to expensive to be
142 |     // worthwhile.
143 |     FlowScope output = flowThrough(source, input);
144 |     Node condition = null;
145 |     FlowScope conditionFlowScope = null;
146 |     BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes = null;
147 | 
148 |     List<DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch>> branchEdges = getCfg().getOutEdges(source);
149 |     List<FlowScope> result = Lists.newArrayListWithCapacity(branchEdges.size());
150 |     for (DiGraphEdge<Node, Branch> branchEdge : branchEdges) {
151 |       Branch branch = branchEdge.getValue();
152 |       FlowScope newScope = output;
153 | 
154 |       switch (branch) {
155 |         case ON_TRUE:
156 |           if (NodeUtil.isForIn(source)) {
157 |             // item is assigned a property name, so its type should be string.
158 |             Node item = source.getFirstChild();
159 |             Node obj = item.getNext();
160 | 
161 |             FlowScope informed = traverse(obj, output.createChildFlowScope());
162 | 
163 |             if (item.isVar()) {
164 |               item = item.getFirstChild();
165 |             }
166 |             if (item.isName()) {
167 |               JSType iterKeyType = getNativeType(STRING_TYPE);
168 |               ObjectType objType = getJSType(obj).dereference();
169 |               JSType objIndexType = objType == null ?
170 |                   null : objType.getIndexType();
171 |               if (objIndexType != null && !objIndexType.isUnknownType()) {
172 |                 JSType narrowedKeyType =
173 |                     iterKeyType.getGreatestSubtype(objIndexType);
174 |                 if (!narrowedKeyType.isEmptyType()) {
175 |                   iterKeyType = narrowedKeyType;
176 |                 }
177 |               }
178 |               redeclareSimpleVar(informed, item, iterKeyType);
179 |             }
180 |             newScope = informed;
181 |             break;
182 |           }
183 | 
184 |           // FALL THROUGH
185 | 
186 |         case ON_FALSE:
187 |           if (condition == null) {
188 |             condition = NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(source);
189 |             if (condition == null && source.isCase()) {
190 |               condition = source;
191 | 
192 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
193 |               // of the loop.
194 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
195 |                 conditionFlowScope = traverse(
196 |                     condition.getFirstChild(), output.createChildFlowScope());
197 |               }
198 |             }
199 |           }
200 | 
201 |           if (condition != null) {
202 |             if (condition.isAnd() ||
203 |                 condition.isOr()) {
204 |               // When handling the short-circuiting binary operators,
205 |               // the outcome scope on true can be different than the outcome
206 |               // scope on false.
207 |               //
208 |               // TODO(nicksantos): The "right" way to do this is to
209 |               // carry the known outcome all the way through the
210 |               // recursive traversal, so that we can construct a
211 |               // different flow scope based on the outcome. However,
212 |               // this would require a bunch of code and a bunch of
213 |               // extra computation for an edge case. This seems to be
214 |               // a "good enough" approximation.
215 | 
216 |               // conditionOutcomes is cached from previous iterations
217 |               // of the loop.
218 |               if (conditionOutcomes == null) {
219 |                 conditionOutcomes = condition.isAnd() ?
220 |                     traverseAnd(condition, output.createChildFlowScope()) :
221 |                     traverseOr(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
222 |               }
223 |               newScope =
224 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
225 |                       condition,
226 |                       conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
227 |                           condition.getType(), branch == Branch.ON_TRUE),
228 |                       branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
229 |             } else {
230 |               // conditionFlowScope is cached from previous iterations
231 |               // of the loop.
232 |               if (conditionFlowScope == null) {
233 |                 conditionFlowScope =
234 |                     traverse(condition, output.createChildFlowScope());
235 |               }
236 |               newScope =
237 |                   reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
238 |                       condition, conditionFlowScope, branch == Branch.ON_TRUE);
239 |             }
240 |           }
241 |           break;
242 |       }
243 | 
244 |       result.add(newScope.optimize());
245 |     }
246 |     return result;
247 |   }

===== M0095 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 331-358
Documentation:

Method code:
331 |   void setPrototypeBasedOn(ObjectType baseType, Node propertyNode) {
332 |     // This is a bit weird. We need to successfully handle these
333 |     // two cases:
334 |     // Foo.prototype = new Bar();
335 |     // and
336 |     // Foo.prototype = {baz: 3};
337 |     // In the first case, we do not want new properties to get
338 |     // added to Bar. In the second case, we do want new properties
339 |     // to get added to the type of the anonymous object.
340 |     //
341 |     // We handle this by breaking it into two cases:
342 |     //
343 |     // In the first case, we create a new PrototypeObjectType and set
344 |     // its implicit prototype to the type being assigned. This ensures
345 |     // that Bar will not get any properties of Foo.prototype, but properties
346 |     // later assigned to Bar will get inherited properly.
347 |     //
348 |     // In the second case, we just use the anonymous object as the prototype.
349 |     if (baseType.hasReferenceName() ||
350 |         isNativeObjectType() ||
351 |         baseType.isFunctionPrototypeType() ||
352 |         !(baseType instanceof PrototypeObjectType)) {
353 | 
354 |       baseType = new PrototypeObjectType(
355 |           registry, this.getReferenceName() + ".prototype", baseType);
356 |     }
357 |     setPrototype((PrototypeObjectType) baseType, propertyNode);
358 |   }

423 |   @Override
424 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
425 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
426 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, true);
427 |     switch (n.getType()) {
428 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
429 |         // normal type checking
430 |         final Scope outerScope = t.getScope();
431 |         final String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
432 |         if (functionPrivateName != null && functionPrivateName.length() > 0 &&
433 |             outerScope.isDeclared(functionPrivateName, false) &&
434 |             // Ideally, we would want to check whether the type in the scope
435 |             // differs from the type being defined, but then the extern
436 |             // redeclarations of built-in types generates spurious warnings.
437 |             !(outerScope.getVar(
438 |                 functionPrivateName).getType() instanceof FunctionType)) {
439 |           report(t, n, FUNCTION_MASKS_VARIABLE, functionPrivateName);
440 |         }
441 | 
442 |         // TODO(user): Only traverse the function's body. The function's
443 |         // name and arguments are traversed by the scope creator, and ideally
444 |         // should not be traversed by the type checker.
445 |         break;
446 |     }
447 |     return true;
448 |   }

/**
   * This function takes a set of GETPROP nodes and produces a string that is
   * each property separated by dots. If the node ultimately under the left
   * sub-tree is not a simple name, this is not a valid qualified name.
   *
   * @return a null if this is not a qualified name, or a dot-separated string

1620 |   public String getQualifiedName() {

1633 |     }

===== M0124 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitParameterList(NodeTraversal,Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1602-1636
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits the parameters of a CALL or a NEW node.
   */
Method code:
1602 |   private void visitParameterList(NodeTraversal t, Node call,
1603 |       FunctionType functionType) {
1604 |     Iterator<Node> arguments = call.children().iterator();
1605 |     arguments.next(); // skip the function name
1606 | 
1607 |     Iterator<Node> parameters = functionType.getParameters().iterator();
1608 |     int ordinal = 0;
1609 |     Node parameter = null;
1610 |     Node argument = null;
1611 |     while (arguments.hasNext() &&
1612 |            (parameters.hasNext() ||
1613 |             parameter != null && parameter.isVarArgs())) {
1614 |       // If there are no parameters left in the list, then the while loop
1615 |       // above implies that this must be a var_args function.
1616 |       if (parameters.hasNext()) {
1617 |         parameter = parameters.next();
1618 |       }
1619 |       argument = arguments.next();
1620 |       ordinal++;
1621 | 
1622 |       validator.expectArgumentMatchesParameter(t, argument,
1623 |           getJSType(argument), getJSType(parameter), call, ordinal);
1624 |     }
1625 | 
1626 |     int numArgs = call.getChildCount() - 1;
1627 |     int minArgs = functionType.getMinArguments();
1628 |     int maxArgs = functionType.getMaxArguments();
1629 |     if (minArgs > numArgs || maxArgs < numArgs) {
1630 |       report(t, call, WRONG_ARGUMENT_COUNT,
1631 |               validator.getReadableJSTypeName(call.getFirstChild(), false),
1632 |               String.valueOf(numArgs), String.valueOf(minArgs),
1633 |               maxArgs != Integer.MAX_VALUE ?
1634 |               " and no more than " + maxArgs + " argument(s)" : "");
1635 |     }
1636 |   }

===== M0133 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.analyze(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 199-227
Documentation:
/**
   * Finds a fixed-point solution. The function has the side effect of replacing
   * the existing node annotations with the computed solutions using {@link
   * com.google.javascript.jscomp.graph.GraphNode#setAnnotation(Annotation)}.
   *
   * <p>Initially, each node's input and output flow state contains the value
   * given by {@link #createInitialEstimateLattice()} (with the exception of the
   * entry node of the graph which takes on the {@link #createEntryLattice()}
   * value. Each node will use the output state of its predecessor and compute a
   * output state according to the instruction. At that time, any nodes that
   * depends on the node's newly modified output value will need to recompute
   * their output state again. Each step will perform a computation at one node
   * until no extra computation will modify any existing output state anymore.
   *
   * @param maxSteps Max number of iterations before the method stops and throw
   *        a {@link MaxIterationsExceededException}. This will prevent the
   *        analysis from going into a infinite loop.
   */
Method code:
199 |   final void analyze(int maxSteps) {
200 |     initialize();
201 |     int step = 0;
202 |     while (!orderedWorkSet.isEmpty()) {
203 |       if (step > maxSteps) {
204 |         throw new MaxIterationsExceededException(
205 |           "Analysis did not terminate after " + maxSteps + " iterations");
206 |       }
207 |       DiGraphNode<N, Branch> curNode = orderedWorkSet.iterator().next();
208 |       orderedWorkSet.remove(curNode);
209 |       joinInputs(curNode);
210 |       if (flow(curNode)) {
211 |         // If there is a change in the current node, we want to grab the list
212 |         // of nodes that this node affects.
213 |         List<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nextNodes = isForward() ?
214 |             cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(curNode) :
215 |             cfg.getDirectedPredNodes(curNode);
216 |         for (DiGraphNode<N, Branch> nextNode : nextNodes) {
217 |           if (nextNode != cfg.getImplicitReturn()) {
218 |             orderedWorkSet.add(nextNode);
219 |           }
220 |         }
221 |       }
222 |       step++;
223 |     }
224 |     if (isForward()) {
225 |       joinInputs(getCfg().getImplicitReturn());
226 |     }
227 |   }

274 |       sourceName = "";

279 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {

===== M0137 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.handleStmtList(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 482-521
Documentation:

Method code:
482 |   private void handleStmtList(Node node) {
483 |     Node parent = node.getParent();
484 |     // Special case, don't add a block of empty CATCH block to the graph.
485 |     if (node.isBlock() && parent != null &&
486 |         parent.isTry() &&
487 |         NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(parent) == node &&
488 |         !NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(node)) {
489 |       return;
490 |     }
491 | 
492 |     // A block transfer control to its first child if it is not empty.
493 |     Node child = node.getFirstChild();
494 | 
495 |     // Function declarations are skipped since control doesn't go into that
496 |     // function (unless it is called)
497 |     while (child != null && child.isFunction()) {
498 |       child = child.getNext();
499 |     }
500 | 
501 |     if (child != null) {
502 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFallThrough(child));
503 |     } else {
504 |       createEdge(node, Branch.UNCOND, computeFollowNode(node, this));
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     // Synthetic blocks
508 |     if (parent != null) {
509 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
510 |         case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
511 |         case Token.CASE:
512 |         case Token.TRY:
513 |           break;
514 |         default:
515 |           if (node.isBlock() && node.isSyntheticBlock()) {
516 |             createEdge(node, Branch.SYN_BLOCK, computeFollowNode(node, this));
517 |           }
518 |           break;
519 |       }
520 |     }
521 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.computeFallThrough(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 786-800
Documentation:
/**
   * Computes the destination node of n when we want to fallthough into the
   * subtree of n. We don't always create a CFG edge into n itself because of
   * DOs and FORs.
   */
Method code:
786 |   static Node computeFallThrough(Node n) {
787 |     switch (n.getType()) {
788 |       case Token.DO:
789 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
790 |       case Token.FOR:
791 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(n)) {
792 |           return n;
793 |         }
794 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getFirstChild());
795 |       case Token.LABEL:
796 |         return computeFallThrough(n.getLastChild());
797 |       default:
798 |         return n;
799 |     }
800 |   }

===== M0143 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getChar()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 337-391
Documentation:

Method code:
337 |   protected int getChar() {
338 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
339 |       cursor++;
340 |       --ungetCursor;
341 |       if (charno == -1) {
342 |         charno = getOffset();
343 |       }
344 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
345 |     }
346 | 
347 |     for(;;) {
348 |       int c;
349 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
350 |         hitEOF = true;
351 |         if (charno == -1) {
352 |           charno = getOffset();
353 |         }
354 |         return EOF_CHAR;
355 |       }
356 |       cursor++;
357 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
358 | 
359 | 
360 |       if (lineEndChar >= 0) {
361 |         if (lineEndChar == '\r' && c == '\n') {
362 |           lineEndChar = '\n';
363 |           continue;
364 |         }
365 |         lineEndChar = -1;
366 |         lineStart = sourceCursor - 1;
367 |         lineno++;
368 |       }
369 | 
370 |       if (c <= 127) {
371 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
372 |           lineEndChar = c;
373 |           c = '\n';
374 |         }
375 |       } else {
376 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
377 |           continue;
378 |         }
379 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
380 |           lineEndChar = c;
381 |           c = '\n';
382 |         }
383 |       }
384 | 
385 |       if (charno == -1) {
386 |         charno = getOffset();
387 |       }
388 | 
389 |       return c;
390 |     }
391 |   }

===== M0151 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 79-140
Documentation:

Method code:
79 |   @Override
80 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType other) {
81 |     if (!(other instanceof ArrowType)) {
82 |       return false;
83 |     }
84 | 
85 |     ArrowType that = (ArrowType) other;
86 | 
87 |     // this.returnType <: that.returnType (covariant)
88 |     if (!this.returnType.isSubtype(that.returnType)) {
89 |       return false;
90 |     }
91 |     // that.paramType[i] <: this.paramType[i] (contravariant)
92 |     // TODO(nicksantos): This is incorrect. It should be invariant.
93 |     // Follow up with closure team on how to fix this without everyone
94 |     // hating on us.
95 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
96 |     Node thatParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
97 |     while (thisParam != null && thatParam != null) {
98 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
99 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
100 |         JSType thatParamType = thatParam.getJSType();
101 |         if (thatParamType == null ||
102 |             !thatParamType.isSubtype(thisParamType)) {
103 |           return false;
104 |         }
105 |       }
106 |       boolean thisIsVarArgs = thisParam.isVarArgs();
107 |       boolean thatIsVarArgs = thatParam.isVarArgs();
108 |       // don't advance if we have variable arguments
109 |       if (!thisIsVarArgs) {
110 |         thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
111 |       }
112 |       if (!thatIsVarArgs) {
113 |         thatParam = thatParam.getNext();
114 |       }
115 |       // both var_args indicates the end
116 |       if (thisIsVarArgs && thatIsVarArgs) {
117 |         thisParam = null;
118 |         thatParam = null;
119 |       }
120 |     }
121 | 
122 |     // Right now, the parser's type system doesn't have a good way
123 |     // to model optional arguments.
124 |     //
125 |     // Suppose we have
126 |     // function f(number, number) {}
127 |     // function g(number) {}
128 |     // If the second arg of f is optional, then f is a subtype of g,
129 |     // but g is not a subtype of f.
130 |     // If the second arg of f is required, then g is a subtype of f,
131 |     // but f is not a subtype of g.
132 |     //
133 |     // Until we model optional params, let's just punt on this.
134 |     // If one type has more arguments than the other, we won't check them.
135 |     //
136 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): This is described in Draft 2 of the ES4 spec,
137 |     // Section 3.4.6: Subtyping Function Types. It seems really
138 |     // strange but I haven't thought a lot about the implementation.
139 |     return true;
140 |   }

278 |     // object can affect subtyping, it's slightly more efficient
279 |     // to register this after defining the property.
280 |     registry.registerPropertyOnType(propertyName, this);
281 | 
282 |     return result;
283 |   }

===== M0159 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1582-1612
Documentation:
/**
     * Resolve any stub delcarations to unknown types if we could not
     * find types for them during traversal.
     */
Method code:
1582 |     void resolveStubDeclarations() {
1583 |       for (StubDeclaration stub : stubDeclarations) {
1584 |         Node n = stub.node;
1585 |         Node parent = n.getParent();
1586 |         String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1587 |         String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1588 |         String ownerName = stub.ownerName;
1589 |         boolean isExtern = stub.isExtern;
1590 | 
1591 |         if (scope.isDeclared(qName, false)) {
1592 |           continue;
1593 |         }
1594 | 
1595 |         // If we see a stub property, make sure to register this property
1596 |         // in the type registry.
1597 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1598 |         ObjectType unknownType = typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1599 |         defineSlot(n, parent, unknownType, true);
1600 | 
1601 |         if (ownerType != null &&
1602 |             (isExtern || ownerType.isFunctionPrototypeType())) {
1603 |           // If this is a stub for a prototype, just declare it
1604 |           // as an unknown type. These are seen often in externs.
1605 |           ownerType.defineInferredProperty(
1606 |               propName, unknownType, n);
1607 |         } else {
1608 |           typeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(
1609 |               propName, ownerType == null ? unknownType : ownerType);
1610 |         }
1611 |       }
1612 |     }

===== M0196 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtypeHelper(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1014-1042
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
1014 |   static boolean isSubtypeHelper(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
1015 |     // unknown
1016 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
1017 |       return true;
1018 |     }
1019 |     // equality
1020 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
1021 |       return true;
1022 |     }
1023 |     // all type
1024 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
1025 |       return true;
1026 |     }
1027 |     // unions
1028 |     if (thatType.isUnionType()) {
1029 |       UnionType union = thatType.toMaybeUnionType();
1030 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
1031 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
1032 |           return true;
1033 |         }
1034 |       }
1035 |     }
1036 |     // proxy types
1037 |     if (thatType instanceof ProxyObjectType) {
1038 |       return thisType.isSubtype(
1039 |           ((ProxyObjectType) thatType).getReferencedTypeInternal());
1040 |     }
1041 |     return false;
1042 |   }

===== M0197 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DataFlowAnalysis.BranchedForwardDataFlowAnalysis.flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DataFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 441-453
Documentation:

Method code:
441 |     @Override
442 |     protected final boolean flow(DiGraphNode<N, Branch> node) {
443 |       BranchedFlowState<L> state = node.getAnnotation();
444 |       List<L> outBefore = state.out;
445 |       state.out = branchedFlowThrough(node.getValue(), state.in);
446 |       Preconditions.checkState(outBefore.size() == state.out.size());
447 |       for (int i = 0; i < outBefore.size(); i++) {
448 |         if (!outBefore.get(i).equals(state.out.get(i))) {
449 |           return true;
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |       return false;
453 |     }

===== M0198 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.typeofClassDefiningName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 137-158
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether the given node is a class-defining name, like
   * "inherits" or "mixin."
   * @return The type of class-defining name, or null.
   */
Method code:
137 |   private SubclassType typeofClassDefiningName(Node callName) {
138 |     // Check if the method name matches one of the class-defining methods.
139 |     String methodName = null;
140 |     if (callName.isGetProp()) {
141 |       methodName = callName.getLastChild().getString();
142 |     } else if (callName.isName()) {
143 |       String name = callName.getString();
144 |       int dollarIndex = name.lastIndexOf('$');
145 |       if (dollarIndex != -1) {
146 |         methodName = name.substring(dollarIndex + 1);
147 |       }
148 |     }
149 | 
150 |     if (methodName != null) {
151 |       if (methodName.equals("inherits")) {
152 |         return SubclassType.INHERITS;
153 |       } else if (methodName.equals("mixin")) {
154 |         return SubclassType.MIXIN;
155 |       }
156 |     }
157 |     return null;
158 |   }

Method code:
1056 |   private JSType getPropertyType(JSType objType, String propName,
1057 |       Node n, FlowScope scope) {
1058 |     // Scopes sometimes contain inferred type info about qualified names.
1059 |     String qualifiedName = n.getQualifiedName();
1060 |     StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(qualifiedName);
1061 |     if (var != null) {
1062 |       JSType varType = var.getType();
1063 |       if (varType != null) {
1064 |         if (varType.equals(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) &&
1065 |             var != syntacticScope.getSlot(qualifiedName)) {
1066 |           // If the type of this qualified name has been checked in this scope,
1067 |           // then use CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE instead to indicate that.
1068 |           return getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1069 |         } else {
1070 |           return varType;
1071 |         }
1072 |       }
1073 |     }
1074 | 
1075 |     JSType propertyType = null;
1076 |     if (objType != null) {
1077 |       propertyType = objType.findPropertyType(propName);
1078 |     }
1079 | 
1080 |     if ((propertyType == null || propertyType.isUnknownType()) &&
1081 |         qualifiedName != null) {
1082 |       // If we find this node in the registry, then we can infer its type.
1083 |       ObjectType regType = ObjectType.cast(registry.getType(qualifiedName));
1084 |       if (regType != null) {
1085 |         propertyType = regType.getConstructor();
1086 |       }
1087 |     }
1088 | 
1089 |     return propertyType;
1090 |   }

/**
   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
429 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
430 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
431 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
432 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();

439 |         // will get an error down the line.
440 |         inputs.add(input);

442 |       }
443 |     }
444 |     return inputs;

===== M0212 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateScopeForTypeChange(FlowScope,Node,JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 440-483
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the scope according to the result of a type change, like
   * an assignment or a type cast.
   */
Method code:
440 |   private void updateScopeForTypeChange(
441 |       FlowScope scope, Node left, JSType leftType, JSType resultType) {
442 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(resultType);
443 |     switch (left.getType()) {
444 |       case Token.NAME:
445 |         String varName = left.getString();
446 |         Var var = syntacticScope.getVar(varName);
447 | 
448 |         // When looking at VAR initializers for declared VARs, we trust
449 |         // the declared type over the type it's being initialized to.
450 |         // This has two purposes:
451 |         // 1) We avoid re-declaring declared variables so that built-in
452 |         //    types defined in externs are not redeclared.
453 |         // 2) When there's a lexical closure like
454 |         //    /** @type {?string} */ var x = null;
455 |         //    function f() { x = 'xyz'; }
456 |         //    the inference will ignore the lexical closure,
457 |         //    which is just wrong. This bug needs to be fixed eventually.
458 |         boolean isVarDeclaration = left.hasChildren();
459 |         if (!isVarDeclaration || var == null || var.isTypeInferred()) {
460 |           redeclareSimpleVar(scope, left, resultType);
461 |         }
462 |         left.setJSType(isVarDeclaration || leftType == null ?
463 |             resultType : null);
464 | 
465 |         if (var != null && var.isTypeInferred()) {
466 |           JSType oldType = var.getType();
467 |           var.setType(oldType == null ?
468 |               resultType : oldType.getLeastSupertype(resultType));
469 |         }
470 |         break;
471 |       case Token.GETPROP:
472 |         String qualifiedName = left.getQualifiedName();
473 |         if (qualifiedName != null) {
474 |           scope.inferQualifiedSlot(left, qualifiedName,
475 |               leftType == null ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : leftType,
476 |               resultType);
477 |         }
478 | 
479 |         left.setJSType(resultType);
480 |         ensurePropertyDefined(left, resultType);
481 |         break;
482 |     }
483 |   }

Method code:
203 |   private boolean isConnectedInDirection(N n1, Predicate<E> edgeMatcher, N n2) {
204 |     // Verify the nodes.
205 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode1 = getNodeOrFail(n1);
206 |     DiGraphNode<N, E> dNode2 = getNodeOrFail(n2);
207 |     for (DiGraphEdge<N, E> outEdge : dNode1.getOutEdges()) {

209 |           edgeMatcher.apply(outEdge.getValue())) {

212 |     }
213 | 
214 |     return false;
215 |   }

===== M0226 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.transformAsString(AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 396-409
Documentation:
/**
     * Transforms the given node and then sets its type to Token.STRING if it
     * was Token.NAME. If its type was already Token.STRING, then quotes it.
     * Used for properties, as the old AST uses String tokens, while the new one
     * uses Name tokens for unquoted strings. For example, in
     * var o = {'a' : 1, b: 2};
     * the string 'a' is quoted, while the name b is turned into a string, but
     * unquoted.
     */
Method code:
396 |     private Node transformAsString(AstNode n) {
397 |       Node ret;
398 |       if (n instanceof Name) {
399 |         ret = transformNameAsString((Name)n);
400 |       } else if (n instanceof NumberLiteral) {
401 |         ret = transformNumberAsString((NumberLiteral)n);
402 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
403 |       } else {
404 |         ret = transform(n);
405 |         ret.putBooleanProp(Node.QUOTED_PROP, true);
406 |       }
407 |       Preconditions.checkState(ret.isString());
408 |       return ret;
409 |     }

===== M0230 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitAssign(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 833-932
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits an assignment <code>lvalue = rvalue</code>. If the
   * <code>lvalue</code> is a prototype modification, we change the schema
   * of the object type it is referring to.
   * @param t the traversal
   * @param assign the assign node
   * (<code>assign.isAssign()</code> is an implicit invariant)
   */
Method code:
833 |   private void visitAssign(NodeTraversal t, Node assign) {
834 |     JSDocInfo info = assign.getJSDocInfo();
835 |     Node lvalue = assign.getFirstChild();
836 |     Node rvalue = assign.getLastChild();
837 | 
838 |     if (lvalue.isGetProp()) {
839 |       Node object = lvalue.getFirstChild();
840 |       JSType objectJsType = getJSType(object);
841 |       String property = lvalue.getLastChild().getString();
842 | 
843 |       // the first name in this getprop refers to an interface
844 |       // we perform checks in addition to the ones below
845 |       if (object.isGetProp()) {
846 |         JSType jsType = getJSType(object.getFirstChild());
847 |         if (jsType.isInterface() &&
848 |             object.getLastChild().getString().equals("prototype")) {
849 |           visitInterfaceGetprop(t, assign, object, property, lvalue, rvalue);
850 |         }
851 |       }
852 | 
853 |       // /** @type ... */object.name = ...;
854 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
855 |         visitAnnotatedAssignGetprop(t, assign,
856 |             info.getType().evaluate(t.getScope(), typeRegistry), object,
857 |             property, rvalue);
858 |         return;
859 |       }
860 | 
861 |       checkEnumAlias(t, info, rvalue);
862 | 
863 |       // object.prototype = ...;
864 |       if (property.equals("prototype")) {
865 |         if (objectJsType != null && objectJsType.isFunctionType()) {
866 |           FunctionType functionType = objectJsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
867 |           if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
868 |             JSType rvalueType = rvalue.getJSType();
869 |             validator.expectObject(t, rvalue, rvalueType,
870 |                 OVERRIDING_PROTOTYPE_WITH_NON_OBJECT);
871 |           }
872 |         } else {
873 |           // TODO(user): might want to flag that
874 |         }
875 |         return;
876 |       }
877 | 
878 |       // object.prototype.property = ...;
879 |       if (object.isGetProp()) {
880 |         Node object2 = object.getFirstChild();
881 |         String property2 = NodeUtil.getStringValue(object.getLastChild());
882 | 
883 |         if ("prototype".equals(property2)) {
884 |           JSType jsType = getJSType(object2);
885 |           if (jsType.isFunctionType()) {
886 |             FunctionType functionType = jsType.toMaybeFunctionType();
887 |             if (functionType.isConstructor() || functionType.isInterface()) {
888 |               checkDeclaredPropertyInheritance(
889 |                   t, assign, functionType, property, info, getJSType(rvalue));
890 |             }
891 |           } else {
892 |             // TODO(user): might want to flag that
893 |           }
894 |           return;
895 |         }
896 |       }
897 | 
898 |       // object.property = ...;
899 |       ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
900 |           objectJsType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
901 |       if (type != null) {
902 |         if (type.hasProperty(property) &&
903 |             !type.isPropertyTypeInferred(property) &&
904 |             !propertyIsImplicitCast(type, property)) {
905 |           validator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(
906 |               t, assign, getJSType(rvalue),
907 |               type.getPropertyType(property), object, property);
908 |         }
909 |         return;
910 |       }
911 |     } else if (lvalue.isName()) {
912 |       // variable with inferred type case
913 |       JSType rvalueType = getJSType(assign.getLastChild());
914 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(lvalue.getString());
915 |       if (var != null) {
916 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
917 |           return;
918 |         }
919 |       }
920 |     }
921 | 
922 |     // fall through case
923 |     JSType leftType = getJSType(lvalue);
924 |     Node rightChild = assign.getLastChild();
925 |     JSType rightType = getJSType(rightChild);
926 |     if (validator.expectCanAssignTo(
927 |             t, assign, rightType, leftType, "assignment")) {
928 |       ensureTyped(t, assign, rightType);
929 |     } else {
930 |       ensureTyped(t, assign);
931 |     }
932 |   }

===== M0234 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 989-991
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks whether {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that}.<p>
   *
   * Subtyping rules:
   * <ul>
   * <li>(unknown) &mdash; every type is a subtype of the Unknown type.</li>
   * <li>(no) &mdash; the No type is a subtype of every type.</li>
   * <li>(no-object) &mdash; the NoObject type is a subtype of every object
   * type (i.e. subtypes of the Object type).</li>
   * <li>(ref) &mdash; a type is a subtype of itself.</li>
   * <li>(union-l) &mdash; A union type is a subtype of a type U if all the
   * union type's constituents are a subtype of U. Formally<br>
   * {@code (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>) &lt;: U} if and only
   * {@code T<sub>k</sub> &lt;: U} for all {@code k &isin; 1..n}.</li>
   * <li>(union-r) &mdash; A type U is a subtype of a union type if it is a
   * subtype of one of the union type's constituents. Formally<br>
   * {@code U &lt;: (T<sub>1</sub>, &hellip;, T<sub>n</sub>)} if and only
   * if {@code U &lt;: T<sub>k</sub>} for some index {@code k}.</li>
   * <li>(objects) &mdash; an Object {@code O<sub>1</sub>} is a subtype
   * of an object {@code O<sub>2</sub>} if it has more properties
   * than {@code O<sub>2</sub>} and all common properties are
   * pairwise subtypes.</li>
   * </ul>
   *
   * @return {@code this &lt;: that}
   */
Method code:
989 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
990 |     return isSubtypeHelper(this, that);
991 |   }

     * Handle typedefs.

1730 |     private void checkForTypedef(
1731 |         NodeTraversal t, Node candidate, JSDocInfo info) {
1732 |       if (info == null || !info.hasTypedefType()) {
1733 |         return;
1734 |       }
1735 | 
1736 |       String typedef = candidate.getQualifiedName();
1737 |       if (typedef == null) {
1738 |         return;
1739 |       }
1740 | 
1741 |       // TODO(nicksantos|user): This is a terrible, terrible hack
1742 |       // to bail out on recusive typedefs. We'll eventually need
1743 |       // to handle these properly.
1744 |       typeRegistry.declareType(typedef, getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1745 |

1747 |       if (realType == null) {
1748 |         compiler.report(
1749 |             JSError.make(
1750 |                 t.getSourceName(), candidate, MALFORMED_TYPEDEF, typedef));
1751 |       }
1752 |

1754 |       if (candidate.isGetProp()) {

1756 |             getNativeType(NO_TYPE), false);

===== M0240 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.parseDirectives(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 457-472
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
457 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
458 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
459 |       Set<String> directives = null;
460 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
461 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
462 |         if (directives == null) {
463 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
464 |         } else {
465 |           directives.add(directive);
466 |         }
467 |       }
468 | 
469 |       if (directives != null) {
470 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
471 |       }
472 |     }

===== M0253 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 443-491
Documentation:

Method code:
443 |   @Override
444 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
445 |     if (JSType.isSubtypeHelper(this, that)) {
446 |       return true;
447 |     }
448 | 
449 |     // Union types
450 |     if (that.isUnionType()) {
451 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
452 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
453 |       return false;
454 |     }
455 | 
456 |     // record types
457 |     if (that.isRecordType()) {
458 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, that.toMaybeRecordType());
459 |     }
460 | 
461 |     // Interfaces
462 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
463 |     // to the interface instance.
464 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
465 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
466 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
467 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
468 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
469 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
470 |           return true;
471 |         }
472 |       }
473 |     }
474 | 
475 |     if (getConstructor() != null && getConstructor().isInterface()) {
476 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : getCtorExtendedInterfaces()) {
477 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
478 |           return true;
479 |         }
480 |       }
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     // other prototype based objects
484 |     if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
485 |       // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
486 |       // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
487 |       // to avoid guessing.
488 |       return true;
489 |     }
490 |     return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
491 |   }

===== M0282 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getStringValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 177-217
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets the value of a node as a String, or null if it cannot be converted.
   * When it returns a non-null String, this method effectively emulates the
   * <code>String()</code> JavaScript cast function.
   */
Method code:
177 |   static String getStringValue(Node n) {
178 |     // TODO(user): regex literals as well.
179 |     switch (n.getType()) {
180 |       case Token.STRING:
181 |         return n.getString();
182 | 
183 |       case Token.NAME:
184 |         String name = n.getString();
185 |         if ("undefined".equals(name)
186 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
187 |             || "NaN".equals(name)) {
188 |           return name;
189 |         }
190 |         break;
191 | 
192 |       case Token.NUMBER:
193 |         return getStringValue(n.getDouble());
194 | 
195 |       case Token.FALSE:
196 |       case Token.TRUE:
197 |       case Token.NULL:
198 |         return Node.tokenToName(n.getType());
199 | 
200 |       case Token.VOID:
201 |         return "undefined";
202 | 
203 |       case Token.NOT:
204 |         TernaryValue child = getPureBooleanValue(n.getFirstChild());
205 |         if (child != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
206 |           return child.toBoolean(true) ? "false" : "true"; // reversed.
207 |         }
208 |         break;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
211 |         return arrayToString(n);
212 | 
213 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
214 |         return "[object Object]";
215 |     }
216 |     return null;
217 |   }

===== M0283 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 925-949
Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve all the unresolved types in the given scope.
   */
Method code:
925 |   public void resolveTypesInScope(StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
926 |     for (NamedType type : unresolvedNamedTypes.get(scope)) {
927 |       type.resolve(reporter, scope);
928 |     }
929 | 
930 |     resolvedNamedTypes.putAll(scope, unresolvedNamedTypes.removeAll(scope));
931 | 
932 |     if (scope != null && scope.getParentScope() == null) {
933 |       // By default, the global "this" type is just an anonymous object.
934 |       // If the user has defined a Window type, make the Window the
935 |       // implicit prototype of "this".
936 |       PrototypeObjectType globalThis = (PrototypeObjectType) getNativeType(
937 |           JSTypeNative.GLOBAL_THIS);
938 |       JSType windowType = getType("Window");
939 |       if (globalThis.isUnknownType()) {
940 |         ObjectType windowObjType = ObjectType.cast(windowType);
941 |         if (windowObjType != null) {
942 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(windowObjType);
943 |         } else {
944 |           globalThis.setImplicitPrototype(
945 |               getNativeObjectType(JSTypeNative.OBJECT_TYPE));
946 |         }
947 |       }
948 |     }
949 |   }
