===== M0287 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 760-802
Documentation:

Method code:
760 |   private FlowScope tightenTypesAfterAssertions(FlowScope scope,
761 |       Node callNode) {
762 |     Node left = callNode.getFirstChild();
763 |     Node firstParam = left.getNext();
764 |     AssertionFunctionSpec assertionFunctionSpec =
765 |         assertionFunctionsMap.get(left.getQualifiedName());
766 |     if (assertionFunctionSpec == null || firstParam == null) {
767 |       return scope;
768 |     }
769 |     Node assertedNode = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedParam(firstParam);
770 |     if (assertedNode == null) {
771 |       return scope;
772 |     }
773 |     JSTypeNative assertedType = assertionFunctionSpec.getAssertedType();
774 |     String assertedNodeName = assertedNode.getQualifiedName();
775 |     // Handle assertions that enforce expressions evaluate to true.
776 |     if (assertedType == null) {
777 |       if (assertedNodeName != null) {
778 |         JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
779 |         JSType narrowed = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
780 |         if (type != narrowed) {
781 |           scope = narrowScope(scope, assertedNode, narrowed);
782 |           callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
783 |         }
784 |       } else if (assertedNode.isAnd() ||
785 |                  assertedNode.isOr()) {
786 |         BooleanOutcomePair conditionOutcomes =
787 |             traverseWithinShortCircuitingBinOp(assertedNode, scope);
788 |         scope = reverseInterpreter.getPreciserScopeKnowingConditionOutcome(
789 |             assertedNode, conditionOutcomes.getOutcomeFlowScope(
790 |                 assertedNode.getType(), true), true);
791 |       }
792 |     } else if (assertedNodeName != null) {
793 |       // Handle assertions that enforce expressions are of a certain type.
794 |       JSType type = getJSType(assertedNode);
795 |       JSType narrowed = type.getGreatestSubtype(getNativeType(assertedType));
796 |       if (type != narrowed) {
797 |         scope = narrowScope(scope, assertedNode, narrowed);
798 |         callNode.setJSType(narrowed);
799 |       }
800 |     }
801 |     return scope;
802 |   }

===== M0289 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 504-539
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
504 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
505 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
506 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.isFunction());
507 | 
508 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
509 | 
510 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
511 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
512 | 
513 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
514 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
515 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
516 |     }
517 | 
518 |     curNode = n;
519 |     pushScope(n);
520 | 
521 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
522 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
523 |       // scope.
524 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
525 |     }
526 | 
527 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
528 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
529 | 
530 |     // Args
531 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
532 | 
533 |     // Body
534 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
535 |             body.isBlock());
536 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
537 | 
538 |     popScope();
539 |   }

===== M0290 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1905-1907
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1905 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1906 |     return n.isFunction() && !isStatement(n);
1907 |   }

===== M0291 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1609-1625
Documentation:
/**
   * TopLevelTypeExpression := TypeExpression
   *     | TypeUnionList
   *
   * We made this rule up, for the sake of backwards compatibility.
   */
Method code:
1609 |   private Node parseTopLevelTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1610 |     Node typeExpr = parseTypeExpression(token);
1611 |     if (typeExpr != null) {
1612 |       // top-level unions are allowed
1613 |       if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1614 |         next();
1615 |         if (match(JsDocToken.PIPE)) {
1616 |           // We support double pipes for backwards-compatibility.
1617 |           next();
1618 |         }
1619 |         skipEOLs();
1620 |         token = next();
1621 |         return parseUnionTypeWithAlternate(token, typeExpr);
1622 |       }
1623 |     }
1624 |     return typeExpr;
1625 |   }

/**
     * Defines a typed variable. The defining node will be annotated with the
     * variable's type of {@link JSTypeNative#UNKNOWN_TYPE} if its type is
     * inferred.
     *
     * Slots may be any variable or any qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.

1036 |             parent.isFunction() ||
1037 |             parent.isVar() ||
1038 |             parent.isParamList() ||
1039 |             parent.isCatch());

1042 |             n.isGetProp() &&
1043 |             (parent.isAssign() ||
1044 |              parent.isExprResult()));
1045 |       }
1046 |       defineSlot(n, parent, n.getQualifiedName(), type, inferred);
1047 |     }

===== M0300 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.LinkedFlowScope(FlatFlowScopeCache,LinkedFlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 63-75
Documentation:

Method code:
63 |   private LinkedFlowScope(FlatFlowScopeCache cache,
64 |       LinkedFlowScope directParent) {
65 |     this.cache = cache;
66 |     if (directParent == null) {
67 |       this.lastSlot = null;
68 |       this.depth = 0;
69 |       this.parent = cache.linkedEquivalent;
70 |     } else {
71 |       this.lastSlot = directParent.lastSlot;
72 |       this.depth = directParent.depth + 1;

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal,JSDocInfo,Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1416-1561
Documentation:
/**
     * Declare the symbol for a qualified name in the global scope.
     *
     * @param info The doc info for this property.
     * @param n A top-level GETPROP node (it should not be contained inside
     *     another GETPROP).
     * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
     * @param rhsValue The node that {@code n} is being initialized to,
     *     or {@code null} if this is a stub declaration.
     */
Method code:
1416 |     void maybeDeclareQualifiedName(NodeTraversal t, JSDocInfo info,
1417 |         Node n, Node parent, Node rhsValue) {
1418 |       Node ownerNode = n.getFirstChild();
1419 |       String ownerName = ownerNode.getQualifiedName();
1420 |       String qName = n.getQualifiedName();
1421 |       String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
1422 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(qName != null && ownerName != null);
1423 | 
1424 |       // Precedence of type information on GETPROPs:
1425 |       // 1) @type annnotation / @enum annotation
1426 |       // 2) ASSIGN to FUNCTION literal
1427 |       // 3) @param/@return annotation (with no function literal)
1428 |       // 4) ASSIGN to something marked @const
1429 |       // 5) ASSIGN to anything else
1430 |       //
1431 |       // 1, 3, and 4 are declarations, 5 is inferred, and 2 is a declaration iff
1432 |       // the function has jsdoc or has not been declared before.
1433 |       //
1434 |       // FUNCTION literals are special because TypedScopeCreator is very smart
1435 |       // about getting as much type information as possible for them.
1436 | 
1437 |       // Determining type for #1 + #2 + #3 + #4
1438 |       JSType valueType = getDeclaredType(t.getSourceName(), info, n, rhsValue);
1439 |       if (valueType == null && rhsValue != null) {
1440 |         // Determining type for #5
1441 |         valueType = rhsValue.getJSType();
1442 |       }
1443 |       // Function prototypes are special.
1444 |       // It's a common JS idiom to do:
1445 |       // F.prototype = { ... };
1446 |       // So if F does not have an explicitly declared super type,
1447 |       // allow F.prototype to be redefined arbitrarily.
1448 |       if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
1449 |         Var qVar = scope.getVar(qName);
1450 |         if (qVar != null) {
1451 |           // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1452 |           // and they assign F.prototype to an object literal,
1453 |           // then they are responsible for making sure that the object literal's
1454 |           // implicit prototype is set up appropriately. We just obey
1455 |           // the @extends tag.
1456 |           ObjectType qVarType = ObjectType.cast(qVar.getType());
1457 |           if (qVarType != null &&
1458 |               rhsValue != null &&
1459 |               rhsValue.isObjectLit()) {
1460 |             typeRegistry.resetImplicitPrototype(
1461 |                 rhsValue.getJSType(), qVarType.getImplicitPrototype());
1462 |           } else if (!qVar.isTypeInferred()) {
1463 |             // If the programmer has declared that F inherits from Super,
1464 |             // and they assign F.prototype to some arbitrary expression,
1465 |             // there's not much we can do. We just ignore the expression,
1466 |             // and hope they've annotated their code in a way to tell us
1467 |             // what props are going to be on that prototype.
1468 |             return;
1469 |           }
1470 |           if (qVar.getScope() == scope) {
1471 |             scope.undeclare(qVar);
1472 |           }
1473 |         }
1474 |       }
1475 | 
1476 |       if (valueType == null) {
1477 |         if (parent.isExprResult()) {
1478 |           stubDeclarations.add(new StubDeclaration(
1479 |               n,
1480 |               t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern(),
1481 |               ownerName));
1482 |         }
1483 | 
1484 |         return;
1485 |       }
1486 | 
1487 |       // NOTE(nicksantos): Determining whether a property is declared or not
1488 |       // is really really obnoxious.
1489 |       //
1490 |       // The problem is that there are two (equally valid) coding styles:
1491 |       //
1492 |       // (function() {
1493 |       //   /* The authoritative definition of goog.bar. */
1494 |       //   goog.bar = function() {};
1495 |       // })();
1496 |       //
1497 |       // function f() {
1498 |       //   goog.bar();
1499 |       //   /* Reset goog.bar to a no-op. */
1500 |       //   goog.bar = function() {};
1501 |       // }
1502 |       //
1503 |       // In a dynamic language with first-class functions, it's very difficult
1504 |       // to know which one the user intended without looking at lots of
1505 |       // contextual information (the second example demonstrates a small case
1506 |       // of this, but there are some really pathological cases as well).
1507 |       //
1508 |       // The current algorithm checks if either the declaration has
1509 |       // jsdoc type information, or @const with a known type,
1510 |       // or a function literal with a name we haven't seen before.
1511 |       boolean inferred = true;
1512 |       if (info != null) {
1513 |         // Determining declaration for #1 + #3 + #4
1514 |         inferred = !(info.hasType()
1515 |             || info.hasEnumParameterType()
1516 |             || (info.isConstant() && valueType != null
1517 |                 && !valueType.isUnknownType())
1518 |             || FunctionTypeBuilder.isFunctionTypeDeclaration(info));
1519 |       }
1520 | 
1521 |       if (inferred) {
1522 |         // Determining declaration for #2
1523 |         inferred = !(rhsValue != null &&
1524 |             rhsValue.isFunction() &&
1525 |             (info != null || !scope.isDeclared(qName, false)));
1526 |       }
1527 | 
1528 |       if (!inferred) {
1529 |         ObjectType ownerType = getObjectSlot(ownerName);
1530 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1531 |           // Only declare this as an official property if it has not been
1532 |           // declared yet.
1533 |           boolean isExtern = t.getInput() != null && t.getInput().isExtern();
1534 |           if ((!ownerType.hasOwnProperty(propName) ||
1535 |                ownerType.isPropertyTypeInferred(propName)) &&
1536 |               ((isExtern && !ownerType.isNativeObjectType()) ||
1537 |                !ownerType.isInstanceType())) {
1538 |             // If the property is undeclared or inferred, declare it now.
1539 |             ownerType.defineDeclaredProperty(propName, valueType, n);
1540 |           }
1541 |         }
1542 | 
1543 |         // If the property is already declared, the error will be
1544 |         // caught when we try to declare it in the current scope.
1545 |         defineSlot(n, parent, valueType, inferred);
1546 |       } else if (rhsValue != null && rhsValue.isTrue()) {
1547 |         // We declare these for delegate proxy method properties.
1548 |         FunctionType ownerType =
1549 |             JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(getObjectSlot(ownerName));
1550 |         if (ownerType != null) {
1551 |           JSType ownerTypeOfThis = ownerType.getTypeOfThis();
1552 |           String delegateName = codingConvention.getDelegateSuperclassName();
1553 |           JSType delegateType = delegateName == null ?
1554 |               null : typeRegistry.getType(delegateName);
1555 |           if (delegateType != null &&
1556 |               ownerTypeOfThis.isSubtype(delegateType)) {
1557 |             defineSlot(n, parent, getNativeType(BOOLEAN_TYPE), true);
1558 |           }
1559 |         }
1560 |       }
1561 |     }

Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1167 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1168 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1169 |       return -1;
1170 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1171 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1172 |     } else {
1173 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1174 |     }
1175 |   }

===== M0325 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectUndeclaredVariable(String,CompilerInput,Node,Node,Var,String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 498-555
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the given variable has not been declared with a type.
   *
   * @param sourceName The name of the source file we're in.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point to.
   * @param parent The parent of {@code n}.
   * @param var The variable that we're checking.
   * @param variableName The name of the variable.
   * @param newType The type being applied to the variable. Mostly just here
   *     for the benefit of the warning.
   */
Method code:
498 |   void expectUndeclaredVariable(String sourceName, CompilerInput input,
499 |       Node n, Node parent, Var var, String variableName, JSType newType) {
500 |     boolean allowDupe = false;
501 |     if (n.isGetProp() ||
502 |         NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
503 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
504 |       if (info == null) {
505 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
506 |       }
507 |       allowDupe =
508 |           info != null && info.getSuppressions().contains("duplicate");
509 |     }
510 | 
511 |     JSType varType = var.getType();
512 | 
513 |     // Only report duplicate declarations that have types. Other duplicates
514 |     // will be reported by the syntactic scope creator later in the
515 |     // compilation process.
516 |     if (varType != null &&
517 |         varType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) &&
518 |         newType != null &&
519 |         newType != typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE)) {
520 |       // If there are two typed declarations of the same variable, that
521 |       // is an error and the second declaration is ignored, except in the
522 |       // case of native types. A null input type means that the declaration
523 |       // was made in TypedScopeCreator#createInitialScope and is a
524 |       // native type. We should redeclare it at the new input site.
525 |       if (var.input == null) {
526 |         Scope s = var.getScope();
527 |         s.undeclare(var);
528 |         s.declare(variableName, n, varType, input, false);
529 | 
530 |         n.setJSType(varType);
531 |         if (parent.isVar()) {
532 |           if (n.getFirstChild() != null) {
533 |             n.getFirstChild().setJSType(varType);
534 |           }
535 |         } else {
536 |           Preconditions.checkState(parent.isFunction());
537 |           parent.setJSType(varType);
538 |         }
539 |       } else {
540 |         // Always warn about duplicates if the overridden type does not
541 |         // match the original type.
542 |         //
543 |         // If the types match, suppress the warning iff there was a @suppress
544 |         // tag, or if the original declaration was a stub.
545 |         if (!(allowDupe ||
546 |               var.getParentNode().isExprResult()) ||
547 |             !newType.equals(varType)) {
548 |           report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, DUP_VAR_DECLARATION,
549 |               variableName, newType.toString(), var.getInputName(),
550 |               String.valueOf(var.nameNode.getLineno()),
551 |               varType.toString()));
552 |         }
553 |       }
554 |     }
555 |   }

118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {

153 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
154 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
155 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
156 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
157 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
158 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
159 |         return processAssignment((Assignment) node);
160 |       case Token.BITNOT:
161 |       case Token.DEC:
162 |       case Token.DELPROP:
163 |       case Token.INC:
164 |       case Token.NEG:
165 |       case Token.NOT:
166 |       case Token.POS:
167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:
186 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
190 |       case Token.COLON:
191 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
192 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
193 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
194 |       case Token.DO:
195 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
196 |       case Token.EMPTY:
197 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
198 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
199 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
200 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
201 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
202 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
203 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
204 |         } else {
205 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
206 |                                           node.getClass() +
207 |                                           " type: " +
208 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
209 |         }
210 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
211 |       case Token.FALSE:
212 |       case Token.NULL:
213 |       case Token.THIS:
214 |       case Token.TRUE:
215 |         return processKeywordLiteral((KeywordLiteral) node);
216 |       case Token.FOR:
217 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
218 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
219 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
220 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
221 |         } else {
222 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
223 |                                           node.getClass() +
224 |                                           " type: " +
225 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
226 |         }
227 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
228 |         return processFunctionNode((FunctionNode) node);
229 |       case Token.GETELEM:
230 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
231 |       case Token.GETPROP:
232 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
233 |       case Token.HOOK:
234 |         return processConditionalExpression((ConditionalExpression) node);
235 |       case Token.IF:
236 |         return processIfStatement((IfStatement) node);
237 |       case Token.LABEL:
238 |         return processLabel((Label) node);
239 |       case Token.LP:
240 |         return processParenthesizedExpression((ParenthesizedExpression) node);
241 |       case Token.NAME:
242 |         return processName((Name) node);
243 |       case Token.NEW:
244 |         return processNewExpression((NewExpression) node);
245 |       case Token.NUMBER:
246 |         return processNumberLiteral((NumberLiteral) node);
247 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
248 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
249 |       case Token.REGEXP:
250 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
251 |       case Token.RETURN:
252 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
253 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
254 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
255 |       case Token.STRING:
256 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
257 |       case Token.SWITCH:
258 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
259 |       case Token.THROW:
260 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
261 |       case Token.TRY:
262 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
263 |       case Token.CONST:
264 |       case Token.VAR:
265 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
266 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
267 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
268 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
269 |         } else {
270 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
271 |                                           node.getClass() +
272 |                                           " type: " +
273 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
274 |         }
275 |       case Token.WHILE:
276 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
277 |       case Token.WITH:
278 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
279 |     }
280 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
281 |   }

===== M0330 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.resolve(ErrorReporter,StaticScope<JSType>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 1078-1091
Documentation:
/**
   * Resolve this type in the given scope.
   *
   * The returned value must be equal to {@code this}, as defined by
   * {@link #isEquivalentTo}. It may or may not be the same object. This method
   * may modify the internal state of {@code this}, as long as it does
   * so in a way that preserves Object equality.
   *
   * For efficiency, we should only resolve a type once per compilation job.
   * For incremental compilations, one compilation job may need the
   * artifacts from a previous generation, so we will eventually need
   * a generational flag instead of a boolean one.
   */
Method code:
1078 |   public final JSType resolve(ErrorReporter t, StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1079 |     if (resolved) {
1080 |       // TODO(nicksantos): Check to see if resolve() looped back on itself.
1081 |       // Preconditions.checkNotNull(resolveResult);
1082 |       if (resolveResult == null) {
1083 |         return registry.getNativeType(JSTypeNative.UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1084 |       }
1085 |       return resolveResult;
1086 |     }
1087 |     resolved = true;
1088 |     resolveResult = resolveInternal(t, scope);
1089 |     resolveResult.setResolvedTypeInternal(resolveResult);
1090 |     return resolveResult;
1091 |   }

===== M0354 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 365-408
Documentation:
/**
   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */
Method code:
365 |   boolean setPrototype(PrototypeObjectType prototype, Node propertyNode) {
366 |     if (prototype == null) {
367 |       return false;
368 |     }
369 |     // getInstanceType fails if the function is not a constructor
370 |     if (isConstructor() && prototype == getInstanceType()) {
371 |       return false;
372 |     }

375 |         ? null : (PrototypeObjectType) prototypeSlot.getType();
376 |     boolean replacedPrototype = oldPrototype != null;

379 |         propertyNode == null ? source : propertyNode);
380 |     prototype.setOwnerFunction(this);
381 | 
382 |     if (oldPrototype != null) {
383 |       // Disassociating the old prototype makes this easier to debug--
384 |       // we don't have to worry about two prototypes running around.
385 |       oldPrototype.setOwnerFunction(null);
386 |     }
387 | 
388 |     if (isConstructor() || isInterface()) {
389 |       FunctionType superClass = getSuperClassConstructor();
390 |       if (superClass != null) {
391 |         superClass.addSubType(this);
392 |       }
393 | 
394 |       if (isInterface()) {
395 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : getExtendedInterfaces()) {
396 |           if (interfaceType.getConstructor() != null) {
397 |             interfaceType.getConstructor().addSubType(this);
398 |           }
399 |         }
400 |       }
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     if (replacedPrototype) {
404 |       clearCachedValues();
405 |     }
406 | 
407 |     return true;
408 |   }

===== M0362 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.checkPropertyAccess(JSType,String,NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1286-1313
Documentation:
/**
   * Emit a warning if we can prove that a property cannot possibly be
   * defined on an object. Note the difference between JS and a strictly
   * statically typed language: we're checking if the property
   * *cannot be defined*, whereas a java compiler would check if the
   * property *can be undefined*.
   */
Method code:
1286 |   private void checkPropertyAccess(JSType childType, String propName,
1287 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1288 |     // If the property type is unknown, check the object type to see if it
1289 |     // can ever be defined. We explicitly exclude CHECKED_UNKNOWN (for
1290 |     // properties where we've checked that it exists, or for properties on
1291 |     // objects that aren't in this binary).
1292 |     JSType propType = getJSType(n);
1293 |     if (propType.equals(typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1294 |       childType = childType.autobox();
1295 |       ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(childType);
1296 |       if (objectType != null) {
1297 |         // We special-case object types so that checks on enums can be
1298 |         // much stricter, and so that we can use hasProperty (which is much
1299 |         // faster in most cases).
1300 |         if (!objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
1301 |             objectType.equals(typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE))) {
1302 |           if (objectType instanceof EnumType) {
1303 |             report(t, n, INEXISTENT_ENUM_ELEMENT, propName);
1304 |           } else {
1305 |             checkPropertyAccessHelper(objectType, propName, t, n);
1306 |           }
1307 |         }
1308 | 
1309 |       } else {
1310 |         checkPropertyAccessHelper(childType, propName, t, n);
1311 |       }
1312 |     }
1313 |   }

===== M0365 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 599-657
Documentation:

Method code:
599 |     @Override
600 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
601 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
602 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
603 |       if (name == null) {
604 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
605 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
606 |           errorReporter.error(
607 |             "unnamed function statement",
608 |             sourceName,
609 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
610 |         }
611 |         name = new Name();
612 |         name.setIdentifier("");
613 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
614 |       }
615 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
616 |       Node newName = transform(name);
617 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
618 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
619 |         // declaration.
620 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
621 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
622 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
623 |         // same line as the function keyword.
624 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
625 |             functionNode.getLp();
626 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
627 |         maybeSetLengthFrom(newName, name);
628 |       }
629 | 
630 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
631 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.PARAM_LIST);
632 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
633 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
634 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
635 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
636 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
637 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
638 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
639 |       if (fnName != null) {
640 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
641 |       } else {
642 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
643 |       }
644 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
645 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
646 | 
647 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
648 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
649 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
650 |       }
651 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
652 | 
653 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
654 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
655 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
656 |      return node;
657 |     }

===== M0371 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.ensurePropertyDefined(Node,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 488-533
Documentation:
/**
   * Defines a property if the property has not been defined yet.
   */
Method code:
488 |   private void ensurePropertyDefined(Node getprop, JSType rightType) {
489 |     String propName = getprop.getLastChild().getString();
490 |     JSType nodeType = getJSType(getprop.getFirstChild());
491 |     ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
492 |         nodeType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
493 |     if (objectType == null) {
494 |       registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, nodeType);
495 |     } else {
496 |       if (ensurePropertyDeclaredHelper(getprop, objectType)) {
497 |         return;
498 |       }
499 | 
500 |       if (!objectType.isPropertyTypeDeclared(propName)) {
501 |         // We do not want a "stray" assign to define an inferred property
502 |         // for every object of this type in the program. So we use a heuristic
503 |         // approach to determine whether to infer the propery.
504 |         //
505 |         // 1) If the property is already defined, join it with the previously
506 |         //    inferred type.
507 |         // 2) If this isn't an instance object, define it.
508 |         // 3) If the property of an object is being assigned in the constructor,
509 |         //    define it.
510 |         // 4) If this is a stub, define it.
511 |         // 5) Otherwise, do not define the type, but declare it in the registry
512 |         //    so that we can use it for missing property checks.
513 |         if (objectType.hasProperty(propName) ||
514 |             !objectType.isInstanceType()) {
515 |           if ("prototype".equals(propName)) {
516 |             objectType.defineDeclaredProperty(
517 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
518 |           } else {
519 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
520 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
521 |           }
522 |         } else {
523 |           if (getprop.getFirstChild().isThis() &&
524 |               getJSType(syntacticScope.getRootNode()).isConstructor()) {
525 |             objectType.defineInferredProperty(
526 |                 propName, rightType, getprop);
527 |           } else {
528 |             registry.registerPropertyOnType(propName, objectType);
529 |           }
530 |         }
531 |       }
532 |     }
533 |   }

===== M0372 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfParameters(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 819-844
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function parameters, type inference will set the type of
   * a function literal argument from the function parameter type.
   */
Method code:
819 |   private void updateTypeOfParameters(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
820 |     int i = 0;
821 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
822 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
823 |       if (i + 1 >= childCount) {
824 |         // TypeCheck#visitParametersList will warn so we bail.
825 |         return;
826 |       }
827 | 
828 |       JSType iParameterType = getJSType(iParameter);
829 |       Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
830 |       JSType iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument);
831 |       inferPropertyTypesToMatchConstraint(iArgumentType, iParameterType);
832 | 
833 |       if (iParameterType.isFunctionType()) {
834 |         FunctionType iParameterFnType = iParameterType.toMaybeFunctionType();
835 | 
836 |         if (iArgument.isFunction() &&
837 |             iArgumentType.isFunctionType() &&
838 |             iArgument.getJSDocInfo() == null) {
839 |           iArgument.setJSType(iParameterFnType);
840 |         }
841 |       }
842 |       i++;
843 |     }
844 |   }

===== M0386 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.checkForClassDefiningCalls(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1296-1363
Documentation:
/**
     * Look for class-defining calls.
     * Because JS has no 'native' syntax for defining classes,
     * this is often very coding-convention dependent and business-logic heavy.
     */
Method code:
1296 |     private void checkForClassDefiningCalls(
1297 |         NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1298 |       SubclassRelationship relationship =
1299 |           codingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(n);
1300 |       if (relationship != null) {
1301 |         FunctionType superCtor = getFunctionType(
1302 |             scope.getVar(relationship.superclassName));
1303 |         FunctionType subCtor = getFunctionType(
1304 |             scope.getVar(relationship.subclassName));
1305 |         if (superCtor != null && superCtor.isConstructor() &&
1306 |             subCtor != null && subCtor.isConstructor()) {
1307 |           ObjectType superClass = superCtor.getInstanceType();
1308 |           ObjectType subClass = subCtor.getInstanceType();
1309 | 
1310 |           // superCtor and subCtor might be structural constructors
1311 |           // (like {function(new:Object)}) so we need to resolve them back
1312 |           // to the original ctor objects.
1313 |           superCtor = superClass.getConstructor();
1314 |           subCtor = subClass.getConstructor();
1315 | 
1316 |           if (relationship.type == SubclassType.INHERITS &&
1317 |               !superClass.isEmptyType() && !subClass.isEmptyType()) {
1318 |             validator.expectSuperType(t, n, superClass, subClass);
1319 |           }
1320 | 
1321 |           if (superCtor != null && subCtor != null) {
1322 |             codingConvention.applySubclassRelationship(
1323 |                 superCtor, subCtor, relationship.type);
1324 |           }
1325 |         }
1326 |       }
1327 | 
1328 |       String singletonGetterClassName =
1329 |           codingConvention.getSingletonGetterClassName(n);
1330 |       if (singletonGetterClassName != null) {
1331 |         ObjectType objectType = ObjectType.cast(
1332 |             typeRegistry.getType(singletonGetterClassName));
1333 |         if (objectType != null) {
1334 |           FunctionType functionType = objectType.getConstructor();
1335 | 
1336 |           if (functionType != null) {
1337 |             FunctionType getterType =
1338 |                 typeRegistry.createFunctionType(objectType);
1339 |             codingConvention.applySingletonGetter(functionType, getterType,
1340 |                 objectType);
1341 |           }
1342 |         }
1343 |       }
1344 | 
1345 |       DelegateRelationship delegateRelationship =
1346 |           codingConvention.getDelegateRelationship(n);
1347 |       if (delegateRelationship != null) {
1348 |         applyDelegateRelationship(delegateRelationship);
1349 |       }
1350 | 
1351 |       ObjectLiteralCast objectLiteralCast =
1352 |           codingConvention.getObjectLiteralCast(t, n);
1353 |       if (objectLiteralCast != null) {
1354 |         ObjectType type = ObjectType.cast(
1355 |             typeRegistry.getType(objectLiteralCast.typeName));
1356 |         if (type != null && type.getConstructor() != null) {
1357 |           setDeferredType(objectLiteralCast.objectNode, type);
1358 |         } else {
1359 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(t.getSourceName(), n,
1360 |                   CONSTRUCTOR_EXPECTED));
1361 |         }
1362 |       }
1363 |     }

===== M0403 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 121-212
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
121 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
122 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
123 |     // just bail out early here.
124 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
125 |       return this;
126 |     }
127 | 
128 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
129 | 
130 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
131 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
132 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
133 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
134 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
135 |     }
136 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
137 |       if (alternate.isUnionType()) {
138 |         UnionType union = alternate.toMaybeUnionType();
139 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
140 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
141 |         }
142 |       } else {
143 |         if (alternates.size() > maxUnionSize) {
144 |           return this;
145 |         }
146 | 
147 |         // Function types are special, because they have their
148 |         // own bizarro sub-lattice. See the commants on
149 |         // FunctionType#supAndInf helper and above at functionTypePosition.
150 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType() && functionTypePosition != -1) {
151 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
152 |           FunctionType other =
153 |               alternates.get(functionTypePosition).toMaybeFunctionType();
154 |           FunctionType supremum =
155 |               alternate.toMaybeFunctionType().supAndInfHelper(other, true);
156 |           alternates.set(functionTypePosition, supremum);
157 |           result = null;
158 |           return this;
159 |         }
160 | 
161 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
162 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
163 |         // one another.
164 |         int currentIndex = 0;
165 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
166 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
167 |           JSType current = it.next();
168 | 
169 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
170 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
171 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
172 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
173 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
174 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
175 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
176 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
177 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
178 |               return this;
179 |             }
180 |           } else {
181 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
182 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
183 |               return this;
184 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
185 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
186 |               it.remove();
187 | 
188 |               if (currentIndex == functionTypePosition) {
189 |                 functionTypePosition = -1;
190 |               } else if (currentIndex < functionTypePosition) {
191 |                 functionTypePosition--;
192 |                 currentIndex--;
193 |               }
194 |             }
195 |           }
196 |           currentIndex++;
197 |         }
198 | 
199 |         if (alternate.isFunctionType()) {
200 |           // See the comments on functionTypePosition above.
201 |           Preconditions.checkState(functionTypePosition == -1);
202 |           functionTypePosition = alternates.size();
203 |         }
204 | 
205 |         alternates.add(alternate);
206 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
207 |       }
208 |     } else {
209 |       result = null;
210 |     }
211 |     return this;
212 |   }

===== M0405 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1658-1697
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeExpression := BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '?' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | '!' BasicTypeExpression
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '?'
   *     | BasicTypeExpression '!'
   *     | '?'
   */
Method code:
1658 |   private Node parseTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1659 |     if (token == JsDocToken.QMARK) {
1660 |       // A QMARK could mean that a type is nullable, or that it's unknown.
1661 |       // We use look-ahead 1 to determine whether it's unknown. Otherwise,
1662 |       // we assume it means nullable. There are 5 cases:
1663 |       // {?} - right curly
1664 |       // {?=} - equals
1665 |       // {function(?, number)} - comma
1666 |       // {function(number, ?)} - right paren
1667 |       // {function(): ?|number} - pipe
1668 |       // I'm not a big fan of using look-ahead for this, but it makes
1669 |       // the type language a lot nicer.
1670 |       token = next();
1671 |       if (token == JsDocToken.COMMA ||
1672 |           token == JsDocToken.EQUALS ||
1673 |           token == JsDocToken.RC ||
1674 |           token == JsDocToken.RP ||
1675 |           token == JsDocToken.PIPE) {
1676 |         restoreLookAhead(token);
1677 |         return newNode(Token.QMARK);
1678 |       }
1679 | 
1680 |       return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, parseBasicTypeExpression(token));
1681 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.BANG) {
1682 |       return wrapNode(Token.BANG, parseBasicTypeExpression(next()));
1683 |     } else {
1684 |       Node basicTypeExpr = parseBasicTypeExpression(token);
1685 |       if (basicTypeExpr != null) {
1686 |         if (match(JsDocToken.QMARK)) {
1687 |           next();
1688 |           return wrapNode(Token.QMARK, basicTypeExpr);
1689 |         } else if (match(JsDocToken.BANG)) {
1690 |           next();
1691 |           return wrapNode(Token.BANG, basicTypeExpr);
1692 |         }
1693 |       }
1694 | 
1695 |       return basicTypeExpr;
1696 |     }
1697 |   }

Original range: 240-266
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect the type to be anything but the null or void type. If the
   * expectation is not met, issue a warning at the provided node's
   * source code position. Note that a union type that includes the
   * void type and at least one other type meets the expectation.
   * @return Whether the expectation was met.
   */
Method code:
240 |   boolean expectNotNullOrUndefined(
241 |       NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type, String msg, JSType expectedType) {
242 |     if (!type.isNoType() && !type.isUnknownType() &&
243 |         type.isSubtype(nullOrUndefined) &&
244 |         !containsForwardDeclaredUnresolvedName(type)) {
245 | 
246 |       // There's one edge case right now that we don't handle well, and
247 |       // that we don't want to warn about.
248 |       // if (this.x == null) {
249 |       //   this.initializeX();
250 |       //   this.x.foo();
251 |       // }
252 |       // In this case, we incorrectly type x because of how we
253 |       // infer properties locally. See issue 109.
254 |       // http://code.google.com/p/closure-compiler/issues/detail?id=109
255 |       //
256 |       // We do not do this inference globally.
257 |       if (n.isGetProp() &&
258 |           !t.inGlobalScope() && type.isNullType()) {
259 |         return true;
260 |       }
261 | 
262 |       mismatch(t, n, msg, type, expectedType);
263 |       return false;
264 |     }
265 |     return true;
266 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder,JSType,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 539-569
Documentation:
/**
   * Add a parameter to the param list.
   * @param builder A builder.
   * @param paramType The parameter type.
   * @param warnedAboutArgList Whether we've already warned about arg ordering
   *     issues (like if optional args appeared before required ones).
   * @param isOptional Is this an optional parameter?
   * @param isVarArgs Is this a var args parameter?
   * @return Whether a warning was emitted.
   */
Method code:
539 |   private boolean addParameter(FunctionParamBuilder builder,
540 |       JSType paramType, boolean warnedAboutArgList,
541 |       boolean isOptional, boolean isVarArgs) {
542 |     boolean emittedWarning = false;
543 |     if (isOptional) {
544 |       // Remembering that an optional parameter has been encountered
545 |       // so that if a non optional param is encountered later, an
546 |       // error can be reported.
547 |       if (!builder.addOptionalParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
548 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
549 |         emittedWarning = true;
550 |       }
551 |     } else if (isVarArgs) {
552 |       if (!builder.addVarArgs(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
553 |         reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
554 |         emittedWarning = true;
555 |       }
556 |     } else {
557 |       if (!builder.addRequiredParams(paramType) && !warnedAboutArgList) {
558 |         // An optional parameter was seen and this argument is not an optional
559 |         // or var arg so it is an error.
560 |         if (builder.hasVarArgs()) {
561 |           reportWarning(VAR_ARGS_MUST_BE_LAST);
562 |         } else {
563 |           reportWarning(OPTIONAL_ARG_AT_END);
564 |         }
565 |         emittedWarning = true;
566 |       }
567 |     }
568 |     return emittedWarning;
569 |   }

1238 |           } else {
1239 |             return createEnumTypeFromNodes(
1240 |                 rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1241 |           }
1242 |         } else if (info.isConstructor() || info.isInterface()) {
1243 |           return createFunctionTypeFromNodes(
1244 |               rValue, lValue.getQualifiedName(), info, lValue);
1245 |         } else {
1246 |           // Check if this is constant, and if it has a known type.
1247 |           if (info.isConstant()) {
1248 |             JSType knownType = null;
1249 |             if (rValue != null) {
1250 |               if (rValue.getJSType() != null
1251 |                   && !rValue.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1252 |                 return rValue.getJSType();
1253 |               } else if (rValue.isOr()) {
1254 |                 // Check for a very specific JS idiom:
1255 |                 // var x = x || TYPE;
1256 |                 // This is used by Closure's base namespace for esoteric
1257 |                 // reasons.
1258 |                 Node firstClause = rValue.getFirstChild();
1259 |                 Node secondClause = firstClause.getNext();
1260 |                 boolean namesMatch = firstClause.isName()
1261 |                     && lValue.isName()
1262 |                     && firstClause.getString().equals(lValue.getString());
1263 |                 if (namesMatch && secondClause.getJSType() != null
1264 |                     && !secondClause.getJSType().isUnknownType()) {
1265 |                   return secondClause.getJSType();
1266 |                 }
1267 |               }
1268 |             }
1269 |           }
1270 |         }
1271 |       }
1272 | 
1273 |       return getDeclaredTypeInAnnotation(sourceName, lValue, info);
1274 |     }

===== M0467 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getJsDocToken()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 59-195
Documentation:
/**
   * Tokenizes JSDoc comments.
   */
Method code:
59 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
60 |   final JsDocToken getJsDocToken() {
61 |     int c;
62 |     stringBufferTop = 0;
63 |     for (;;) {
64 |       // eat white spaces
65 |       for (;;) {
66 |         charno = -1;
67 |         c = getChar();
68 |         if (c == EOF_CHAR) {
69 |           return JsDocToken.EOF;
70 |         } else if (c == '\n') {
71 |           return JsDocToken.EOL;
72 |         } else if (!isJSSpace(c)) {
73 |           break;
74 |         }
75 |       }
76 | 
77 |       switch (c) {
78 |         // annotation, e.g. @type or @constructor
79 |         case '@':
80 |           do {
81 |             c = getChar();
82 |             if (isAlpha(c)) {
83 |               addToString(c);
84 |             } else {
85 |               ungetChar(c);
86 |               this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
87 |               stringBufferTop = 0;
88 |               return JsDocToken.ANNOTATION;
89 |             }
90 |           } while (true);
91 | 
92 |         case '*':
93 |           if (matchChar('/')) {
94 |             return JsDocToken.EOC;
95 |           } else {
96 |             return JsDocToken.STAR;
97 |           }
98 | 
99 |         case ',':
100 |           return JsDocToken.COMMA;
101 | 
102 |         case '>':
103 |           return JsDocToken.GT;
104 | 
105 |         case '(':
106 |           return JsDocToken.LP;
107 | 
108 |         case ')':
109 |           return JsDocToken.RP;
110 | 
111 |         case '{':
112 |           return JsDocToken.LC;
113 | 
114 |         case '}':
115 |           return JsDocToken.RC;
116 | 
117 |         case '[':
118 |           return JsDocToken.LB;
119 | 
120 |         case ']':
121 |           return JsDocToken.RB;
122 | 
123 |         case '?':
124 |           return JsDocToken.QMARK;
125 | 
126 |         case '!':
127 |           return JsDocToken.BANG;
128 | 
129 |         case ':':
130 |           return JsDocToken.COLON;
131 | 
132 |         case '=':
133 |           return JsDocToken.EQUALS;
134 | 
135 |         case '|':
136 |           matchChar('|');
137 |           return JsDocToken.PIPE;
138 | 
139 |         case '.':
140 |           c = getChar();
141 |           if (c == '<') {
142 |             return JsDocToken.LT;
143 |           } else {
144 |             if (c == '.') {
145 |               c = getChar();
146 |               if (c == '.') {
147 |                 return JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS;
148 |               } else {
149 |                 addToString('.');
150 |               }
151 |             }
152 |             // we may backtrack across line boundary
153 |             ungetBuffer[ungetCursor++] = c;
154 |             c = '.';
155 |           }
156 |           // fall through
157 | 
158 |         default: {
159 |           // recognize a jsdoc string but discard last . if it is followed by
160 |           // a non-jsdoc comment char, e.g. Array.<
161 |           int c1 = c;
162 |           addToString(c);
163 |           int c2 = getChar();
164 |           if (!isJSDocString(c2)) {
165 |             ungetChar(c2);
166 |             this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
167 |             stringBufferTop = 0;
168 |             return JsDocToken.STRING;
169 |           } else {
170 |             do {
171 |               c1 = c2;
172 |               c2 = getChar();
173 |               if (c1 == '.' && c2 == '<') {
174 |                 ungetChar(c2);
175 |                 ungetChar(c1);
176 |                 this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
177 |                 stringBufferTop = 0;
178 |                 return JsDocToken.STRING;
179 |               } else {
180 |                 if (isJSDocString(c2)) {
181 |                   addToString(c1);
182 |                 } else {
183 |                   ungetChar(c2);
184 |                   addToString(c1);
185 |                   this.string = getStringFromBuffer();
186 |                   stringBufferTop = 0;
187 |                   return JsDocToken.STRING;
188 |                 }
189 |               }
190 |             } while (true);
191 |           }
192 |         }
193 |       }
194 |     }
195 |   }

     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */

164 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {

===== M0480 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.traverseName(Node,FlowScope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 575-614
Documentation:

Method code:
575 |   private FlowScope traverseName(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
576 |     String varName = n.getString();
577 |     Node value = n.getFirstChild();
578 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
579 |     if (value != null) {
580 |       scope = traverse(value, scope);
581 |       updateScopeForTypeChange(scope, n, n.getJSType() /* could be null */,
582 |           getJSType(value));
583 |       return scope;
584 |     } else {
585 |       StaticSlot<JSType> var = scope.getSlot(varName);
586 |       if (var != null) {
587 |         // There are two situations where we don't want to use type information
588 |         // from the scope, even if we have it.
589 | 
590 |         // 1) The var is escaped in a weird way, e.g.,
591 |         // function f() { var x = 3; function g() { x = null } (x); }
592 |         boolean isInferred = var.isTypeInferred();
593 |         boolean unflowable = isInferred &&
594 |             isUnflowable(syntacticScope.getVar(varName));
595 | 
596 |         // 2) We're reading type information from another scope for an
597 |         // inferred variable.
598 |         // var t = null; function f() { (t); }
599 |         boolean nonLocalInferredSlot =
600 |             isInferred &&
601 |             syntacticScope.getParent() != null &&
602 |             var == syntacticScope.getParent().getSlot(varName);
603 | 
604 |         if (!unflowable && !nonLocalInferredSlot) {
605 |           type = var.getType();
606 |           if (type == null) {
607 |             type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
608 |           }
609 |         }
610 |       }
611 |     }
612 |     n.setJSType(type);
613 |     return scope;
614 |   }

===== M0519 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseTypeName(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1736-1771
Documentation:
/**
   * TypeName := NameExpression | NameExpression TypeApplication
   * TypeApplication := '.<' TypeExpressionList '>'
   * TypeExpressionList := TypeExpression // a white lie
   */
Method code:
1736 |   private Node parseTypeName(JsDocToken token) {
1737 |     if (token != JsDocToken.STRING) {
1738 |       return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1739 |     }
1740 | 
1741 |     String typeName = stream.getString();
1742 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1743 |     int charno = stream.getCharno();
1744 |     while (match(JsDocToken.EOL) &&
1745 |         typeName.charAt(typeName.length() - 1) == '.') {
1746 |       skipEOLs();
1747 |       if (match(JsDocToken.STRING)) {
1748 |         next();
1749 |         typeName += stream.getString();
1750 |       }
1751 |     }
1752 | 
1753 |     Node typeNameNode = newStringNode(typeName, lineno, charno);
1754 | 
1755 |     if (match(JsDocToken.LT)) {
1756 |       next();
1757 |       skipEOLs();
1758 |       Node memberType = parseTypeExpressionList(next());
1759 |       if (memberType != null) {
1760 |         typeNameNode.addChildToFront(memberType);
1761 | 
1762 |         skipEOLs();
1763 |         if (!match(JsDocToken.GT)) {
1764 |           return reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.gt");
1765 |         }
1766 | 
1767 |         next();
1768 |       }
1769 |     }
1770 |     return typeNameNode;
1771 |   }

===== M0521 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ArrowType.hasEqualParameters(ArrowType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ArrowType.java
Original range: 146-169
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if our parameter spec is equal to {@code that}'s parameter
   *     spec.
   */
Method code:
146 |   boolean hasEqualParameters(ArrowType that) {
147 |     Node thisParam = parameters.getFirstChild();
148 |     Node otherParam = that.parameters.getFirstChild();
149 |     while (thisParam != null && otherParam != null) {
150 |       JSType thisParamType = thisParam.getJSType();
151 |       JSType otherParamType = otherParam.getJSType();
152 |       if (thisParamType != null) {
153 |         // Both parameter lists give a type for this param, it should be equal
154 |         if (otherParamType != null &&
155 |             !thisParamType.isEquivalentTo(otherParamType)) {
156 |           return false;
157 |         }
158 |       } else {
159 |         if (otherParamType != null) {
160 |           return false;
161 |         }
162 |       }
163 |       thisParam = thisParam.getNext();
164 |       otherParam = otherParam.getNext();
165 |     }
166 |     // One of the parameters is null, so the types are only equal if both
167 |     // parameter lists are null (they are equal).
168 |     return thisParam == otherParam;
169 |   }

   * Determines if the subtree might throw an exception.
   */

900 |   public static boolean mayThrowException(Node n) {
901 |     switch (n.getType()) {
902 |       case Token.CALL:
903 |       case Token.GETPROP:
904 |       case Token.GETELEM:
905 |       case Token.THROW:
906 |       case Token.NEW:
907 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
908 |       case Token.INC:
909 |       case Token.DEC:

911 |         return true;
912 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
913 |         return false;
914 |     }
915 |     for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
916 |       if (!ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(c) && mayThrowException(c)) {
917 |         return true;
918 |       }

920 |     return false;
921 |   }

===== M0547 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeInference.updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node,FunctionType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeInference.java
Original range: 851-938
Documentation:
/**
   * For functions with function(this: T, ...) and T as parameters, type
   * inference will set the type of this on a function literal argument to the
   * the actual type of T.
   */
Method code:
851 |   private void updateTypeOfThisOnClosure(Node n, FunctionType fnType) {
852 |     // TODO(user): Make the template logic more general.
853 | 
854 |     if (fnType.getTemplateTypeName() == null) {
855 |       return;
856 |     }
857 | 
858 |     int i = 0;
859 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
860 |     // Find the parameter whose type is the template type.
861 |     for (Node iParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
862 |       JSType iParameterType =
863 |           getJSType(iParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
864 |       if (iParameterType.isTemplateType()) {
865 |         // Find the actual type of this argument.
866 |         ObjectType iArgumentType = null;
867 |         if (i + 1 < childCount) {
868 |           Node iArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(i + 1);
869 |           iArgumentType = getJSType(iArgument)
870 |               .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined()
871 |               .collapseUnion()
872 |               .toObjectType();
873 |           if (iArgumentType == null) {
874 |             compiler.report(
875 |                 JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(iArgument), iArgument,
876 |                     TEMPLATE_TYPE_NOT_OBJECT_TYPE,
877 |                     getJSType(iArgument).toString()));
878 |             return;
879 |           }
880 |         }
881 | 
882 |         // Find the parameter whose type is function(this: T, ...)
883 |         boolean foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = false;
884 |         int j = 0;
885 |         for (Node jParameter : fnType.getParameters()) {
886 |           JSType jParameterType =
887 |               getJSType(jParameter).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
888 |           if (jParameterType.isFunctionType()) {
889 |             FunctionType jParameterFnType = jParameterType.toMaybeFunctionType();
890 |             if (jParameterFnType.getTypeOfThis().equals(iParameterType)) {
891 |               foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter = true;
892 |               // Find the actual type of the this argument.
893 |               if (j + 1 >= childCount) {
894 |                 // TypeCheck#visitParameterList will warn so we bail.
895 |                 return;
896 |               }
897 |               Node jArgument = n.getChildAtIndex(j + 1);
898 |               JSType jArgumentType = getJSType(jArgument);
899 |               if (jArgument.isFunction() &&
900 |                   jArgumentType.isFunctionType()) {
901 |                 if (iArgumentType != null &&
902 |                     // null and undefined get filtered out above.
903 |                     !iArgumentType.isNoType()) {
904 |                   // If it's an function expression, update the type of this
905 |                   // using the actual type of T.
906 |                   FunctionType jArgumentFnType = jArgumentType.toMaybeFunctionType();
907 |                   if (jArgumentFnType.getTypeOfThis().isUnknownType()) {
908 |                     // The new type will be picked up when we traverse the inner
909 |                     // function.
910 |                     jArgument.setJSType(
911 |                         registry.createFunctionTypeWithNewThisType(
912 |                             jArgumentFnType, iArgumentType));
913 |                   }
914 |                 } else {
915 |                   // Warn if the anonymous function literal references this.
916 |                   if (NodeUtil.referencesThis(
917 |                           NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(jArgument))) {
918 |                     compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
919 |                         FUNCTION_LITERAL_UNDEFINED_THIS));
920 |                   }
921 |                 }
922 |               }
923 |               // TODO(user): Add code to TypeCheck to check that the
924 |               // types of the arguments match.
925 |             }
926 |           }
927 |           j++;
928 |         }
929 | 
930 |         if (!foundTemplateTypeOfThisParameter) {
931 |           compiler.report(JSError.make(NodeUtil.getSourceName(n), n,
932 |               TEMPLATE_TYPE_OF_THIS_EXPECTED));
933 |           return;
934 |         }
935 |       }
936 |       i++;
937 |     }
938 |   }

===== M0556 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 766-790
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
766 |   @Override
767 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
768 |     FunctionType that =
769 |         JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(otherType);
770 |     if (that == null) {
771 |       return false;
772 |     }
773 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
774 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
775 |         return this == that;
776 |       }
777 |       return false;
778 |     }
779 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
780 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
781 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
782 |       }
783 |       return false;
784 |     }
785 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
786 |       return false;
787 |     }
788 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
789 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
790 |   }

1127 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1128 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(!ctor.isUnknownType());
1129 | 
1130 |     // The type system should notice inheritance cycles on its own
1131 |     // and break the cycle.
1132 |     while (true) {
1133 |       ObjectType maybeSuperInstanceType =
1134 |           ctor.getPrototype().getImplicitPrototype();
1135 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType == null) {
1136 |         return false;
1137 |       }
1138 |       if (maybeSuperInstanceType.isUnknownType() ||
1139 |           maybeSuperInstanceType.isEmptyType()) {
1140 |         return true;
1141 |       }
1142 |       ctor = maybeSuperInstanceType.getConstructor();
1143 |       if (ctor == null) {
1144 |         return false;
1145 |       }
1146 |       Preconditions.checkState(ctor.isConstructor() || ctor.isInterface());
1147 |     }
1148 |   }

===== M0561 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitName(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1212-1241
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a NAME node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   * @return whether the node is typeable or not
   */
Method code:
1212 |   boolean visitName(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1213 |     // At this stage, we need to determine whether this is a leaf
1214 |     // node in an expression (which therefore needs to have a type
1215 |     // assigned for it) versus some other decorative node that we
1216 |     // can safely ignore.  Function names, arguments (children of LP nodes) and
1217 |     // variable declarations are ignored.
1218 |     // TODO(user): remove this short-circuiting in favor of a
1219 |     // pre order traversal of the FUNCTION, CATCH, LP and VAR nodes.
1220 |     int parentNodeType = parent.getType();
1221 |     if (parentNodeType == Token.FUNCTION ||
1222 |         parentNodeType == Token.CATCH ||
1223 |         parentNodeType == Token.PARAM_LIST ||
1224 |         parentNodeType == Token.VAR) {
1225 |       return false;
1226 |     }
1227 | 
1228 |     JSType type = n.getJSType();
1229 |     if (type == null) {
1230 |       type = getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1231 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
1232 |       if (var != null) {
1233 |         JSType varType = var.getType();
1234 |         if (varType != null) {
1235 |           type = varType;
1236 |         }
1237 |       }
1238 |     }
1239 |     ensureTyped(t, n, type);
1240 |     return true;
1241 |   }

===== M0568 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1703-1729
Documentation:
/**
   * BasicTypeExpression := '*' | 'null' | 'undefined' | TypeName
   *     | FunctionType | UnionType | RecordType | ArrayType
   */
Method code:
1703 |   private Node parseBasicTypeExpression(JsDocToken token) {
1704 |     if (token == JsDocToken.STAR) {
1705 |       return newNode(Token.STAR);
1706 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LB) {
1707 |       skipEOLs();
1708 |       return parseArrayType(next());
1709 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
1710 |       skipEOLs();
1711 |       return parseRecordType(next());
1712 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.LP) {
1713 |       skipEOLs();
1714 |       return parseUnionType(next());
1715 |     } else if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
1716 |       String string = stream.getString();
1717 |       if ("function".equals(string)) {
1718 |         skipEOLs();
1719 |         return parseFunctionType(next());
1720 |       } else if ("null".equals(string) || "undefined".equals(string)) {
1721 |         return newStringNode(string);
1722 |       } else {
1723 |         return parseTypeName(token);
1724 |       }
1725 |     }
1726 | 
1727 |     restoreLookAhead(token);
1728 |     return reportGenericTypeSyntaxWarning();
1729 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a GETPROP node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of <code>n</code>
   */
Method code:
1251 |   private void visitGetProp(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
1252 |     // GETPROP nodes have an assigned type on their node by the scope creator
1253 |     // if this is an enum declaration. The only namespaced enum declarations
1254 |     // that we allow are of the form object.name = ...;
1255 |     if (n.getJSType() != null && parent.isAssign()) {
1256 |       return;
1257 |     }
1258 | 
1259 |     // obj.prop or obj.method()
1260 |     // Lots of types can appear on the left, a call to a void function can
1261 |     // never be on the left. getPropertyType will decide what is acceptable
1262 |     // and what isn't.
1263 |     Node property = n.getLastChild();
1264 |     Node objNode = n.getFirstChild();
1265 |     JSType childType = getJSType(objNode);
1266 | 
1267 |     // TODO(user): remove in favor of flagging every property access on
1268 |     // non-object.
1269 |     if (!validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(t, n, childType,
1270 |             "No properties on this expression", getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE))) {
1271 |       ensureTyped(t, n);
1272 |       return;
1273 |     }
1274 | 
1275 |     checkPropertyAccess(childType, property.getString(), t, n);
1276 |     ensureTyped(t, n);
1277 |   }

288 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
289 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
290 |       return;
291 |     }
292 | 
293 |     try {
294 |       Node scopeRoot = roots.get(0).getParent();
295 |       Preconditions.checkState(scopeRoot != null);
296 | 
297 |       inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(scopeRoot);
298 |       sourceName = "";
299 |       curNode = scopeRoot;
300 |       pushScope(scopeRoot);
301 | 
302 |       for (Node root : roots) {
303 |         Preconditions.checkState(root.getParent() == scopeRoot);
304 |         traverseBranch(root, scopeRoot);
305 |       }
306 | 
307 |       popScope();
308 |     } catch (Exception unexpectedException) {
309 |       throwUnexpectedException(unexpectedException);
310 |     }
311 |   }

===== M0602 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.isJSSpace(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 310-317
Documentation:
/* As defined in ECMA.  jsscan.c uses C isspace() (which allows
   * \v, I think.)  note that code in getChar() implicitly accepts
   * '\r' == \u000D as well.
   */
Method code:
310 |   static boolean isJSSpace(int c) {
311 |     if (c <= 127) {
312 |       return c == 0x20 || c == 0x9 || c == 0xC || c == 0xB;
313 |     } else {
314 |       return c == 0xA0
315 |           || Character.getType((char)c) == Character.SPACE_SEPARATOR;
316 |     }
317 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Constructs a data flow analysis.
   *
   * <p>Typical usage
   * <pre>
   * DataFlowAnalysis dfa = ...
   * dfa.analyze();
   * </pre>
   *
   * {@link #analyze()} annotates the result to the control flow graph by
   * means of {@link DiGraphNode#setAnnotation} without any
   * modification of the graph itself. Additional calls to {@link #analyze()}
   * recomputes the analysis which can be useful if the control flow graph
   * has been modified.
   *
   * @param targetCfg The control flow graph object that this object performs
   *     on. Modification of the graph requires a separate call to
   *     {@link #analyze()}.
   *
   * @see #analyze()
   */
Method code:
118 |   DataFlowAnalysis(ControlFlowGraph<N> targetCfg, JoinOp<L> joinOp) {
119 |     this.cfg = targetCfg;
120 |     this.joinOp = joinOp;
121 |     Comparator<DiGraphNode<N, Branch>> nodeComparator =
122 |       cfg.getOptionalNodeComparator(isForward());
123 |     if (nodeComparator != null) {
124 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newTreeSet(nodeComparator);
125 |     } else {
126 |       this.orderedWorkSet = Sets.newLinkedHashSet();
127 |     }
128 |   }

740 |   private FlowScope traverseCall(Node n, FlowScope scope) {

742 |

744 |     JSType functionType = getJSType(left).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();

249 |   private FlowScope traverse(Node n, FlowScope scope) {
250 |     switch (n.getType()) {

253 |         break;

272 |

274 |         scope = traverseHook(n, scope);
275 |         break;
276 | 
277 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
278 |         scope = traverseObjectLiteral(n, scope);
279 |         break;

286 |         scope = traverseNew(n, scope);
287 |         break;
288 | 
289 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
290 |       case Token.ADD:
291 |         scope = traverseAdd(n, scope);
292 |         break;
293 | 
294 |       case Token.POS:
295 |       case Token.NEG:
296 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);  // Find types.
297 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));

312 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
313 |       case Token.URSH:
314 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
315 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
316 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
317 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
318 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
319 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
320 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
321 |       case Token.DIV:
322 |       case Token.MOD:
323 |       case Token.BITAND:
324 |       case Token.BITXOR:
325 |       case Token.BITOR:
326 |       case Token.MUL:
327 |       case Token.SUB:
328 |       case Token.DEC:
329 |       case Token.INC:
330 |       case Token.BITNOT:
331 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);
332 |         n.setJSType(getNativeType(NUMBER_TYPE));

336 |         scope = traverse(n.getFirstChild(), scope);

343 |         break;

346 |         scope = traverseChildren(n, scope);

348 |         break;

385 |         break;

387 |       case Token.CATCH:
388 |         scope = traverseCatch(n, scope);
389 |         break;
390 |     }
391 |     if (n.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
392 |       JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
393 |       if (info != null && info.hasType()) {
394 |         JSType castType = info.getType().evaluate(syntacticScope, registry);
395 |
