Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3056-3069
Documentation:
/** Find the l-value that the given r-value is being assigned to. */
Method code:
3056 |   static Node getBestLValue(Node n) {
3057 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
3058 |     boolean isFunctionDeclaration = isFunctionDeclaration(n);
3059 |     if (isFunctionDeclaration) {
3060 |       return n.getFirstChild();
3061 |     } else if (parent.isName()) {
3062 |       return parent;
3063 |     } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3064 |       return parent.getFirstChild();
3065 |     } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3066 |       return parent;
3067 |     }
3068 |     return null;
3069 |   }

===== M0658 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 820-847
Documentation:
/**
   * Connects cfgNode to the proper CATCH block if target subtree might throw
   * an exception. If there are FINALLY blocks reached before a CATCH, it will
   * make the corresponding entry in finallyMap.
   */
Method code:
820 |   private void connectToPossibleExceptionHandler(Node cfgNode, Node target) {
821 |     if (mayThrowException(target) && !exceptionHandler.isEmpty()) {
822 |       Node lastJump = cfgNode;
823 |       for (Node handler : exceptionHandler) {
824 |         if (handler.isFunction()) {
825 |           return;
826 |         }
827 |         Preconditions.checkState(handler.isTry());
828 |         Node catchBlock = NodeUtil.getCatchBlock(handler);
829 | 
830 |         if (!NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(catchBlock)) { // No catch but a FINALLY.
831 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
832 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, handler.getLastChild());
833 |           } else {
834 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, handler.getLastChild());
835 |           }
836 |         } else { // Has a catch.
837 |           if (lastJump == cfgNode) {
838 |             createEdge(cfgNode, Branch.ON_EX, catchBlock);
839 |             return;
840 |           } else {
841 |             finallyMap.put(lastJump, catchBlock);
842 |           }
843 |         }
844 |         lastJump = handler;
845 |       }
846 |     }
847 |   }

3086 |   static String getBestLValueName(@Nullable Node lValue) {
3087 |     if (lValue == null || lValue.getParent() == null) {
3088 |       return null;

3099 |     }

3101 |   }

===== M0689 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getBestJSDocInfo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 3032-3053
Documentation:
/** Find the best JSDoc for the given node. */
Method code:
3032 |   static JSDocInfo getBestJSDocInfo(Node n) {
3033 |     JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
3034 |     if (info == null) {
3035 |       Node parent = n.getParent();
3036 |       if (parent == null) {
3037 |         return null;
3038 |       }
3039 | 
3040 |       if (parent.isName()) {
3041 |         return getBestJSDocInfo(parent);
3042 |       } else if (parent.isAssign()) {
3043 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
3044 |       } else if (isObjectLitKey(parent, parent.getParent())) {
3045 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
3046 |       } else if (parent.isFunction()) {
3047 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
3048 |       } else if (parent.isVar() && parent.hasOneChild()) {
3049 |         info = parent.getJSDocInfo();
3050 |       }
3051 |     }
3052 |     return info;
3053 |   }

===== M0693 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 196-215
Documentation:
/**
   * Given an entry node, find all the nodes reachable from that node
   * and prioritize them.
   */
Method code:
196 |   private void prioritizeFromEntryNode(DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> entry) {
197 |     PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> worklist =
198 |         new PriorityQueue<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>>(10, priorityComparator);
199 |     worklist.add(entry);
200 | 
201 |     while (!worklist.isEmpty()) {
202 |       DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> current = worklist.remove();
203 |       if (nodePriorities.containsKey(current)) {
204 |         continue;
205 |       }
206 | 
207 |       nodePriorities.put(current, ++priorityCounter);
208 | 
209 |       List<DiGraphNode<Node, Branch>> successors =
210 |           cfg.getDirectedSuccNodes(current);
211 |       for (DiGraphNode<Node, Branch> candidate : successors) {
212 |         worklist.add(candidate);
213 |       }
214 |     }
215 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 219-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
219 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
220 |     if (isAllType) {
221 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
222 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
223 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
224 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
225 |       } else {
226 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
227 |       }
228 |     } else {
229 |       int size = alternates.size();
230 |       if (size > maxUnionSize) {
231 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
232 |       } else if (size > 1) {
233 |         return null;
234 |       } else if (size == 1) {
235 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
236 |       } else {
237 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 |   }

Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter types from the list of argument names and
   * the doc info.
   */
Method code:
418 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferParameterTypes(@Nullable Node argsParent,
419 |       @Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
420 |     if (argsParent == null) {
421 |       if (info == null) {
422 |         return this;
423 |       } else {
424 |         return inferParameterTypes(info);
425 |       }
426 |     }
427 | 
428 |     // arguments
429 |     Node oldParameterType = null;
430 |     if (parametersNode != null) {
431 |       oldParameterType = parametersNode.getFirstChild();
432 |     }
433 | 
434 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
435 |     boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
436 |     Set<String> allJsDocParams = (info == null) ?
437 |         Sets.<String>newHashSet() :
438 |         Sets.newHashSet(info.getParameterNames());
439 |     boolean foundTemplateType = false;
440 |     for (Node arg : argsParent.children()) {
441 |       String argumentName = arg.getString();
442 |       allJsDocParams.remove(argumentName);
443 | 
444 |       // type from JSDocInfo
445 |       JSType parameterType = null;
446 |       boolean isOptionalParam = isOptionalParameter(arg, info);
447 |       boolean isVarArgs = isVarArgsParameter(arg, info);
448 |       if (info != null && info.hasParameterType(argumentName)) {
449 |         parameterType =
450 |             info.getParameterType(argumentName).evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
451 |       } else if (oldParameterType != null &&
452 |           oldParameterType.getJSType() != null) {
453 |         parameterType = oldParameterType.getJSType();
454 |         isOptionalParam = oldParameterType.isOptionalArg();
455 |         isVarArgs = oldParameterType.isVarArgs();
456 |       } else {
457 |         parameterType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
458 |       }
459 | 
460 |       if (templateTypeName != null &&
461 |           parameterType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined().isTemplateType()) {
462 |         if (foundTemplateType) {
463 |           reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_DUPLICATED, fnName);
464 |         }
465 |         foundTemplateType = true;
466 |       }
467 |       warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
468 |           builder, parameterType, warnedAboutArgList,
469 |           isOptionalParam,
470 |           isVarArgs);
471 | 
472 |       if (oldParameterType != null) {
473 |         oldParameterType = oldParameterType.getNext();
474 |       }
475 |     }
476 | 
477 |     if (templateTypeName != null && !foundTemplateType) {
478 |       reportError(TEMPLATE_TYPE_EXPECTED, fnName);
479 |     }
480 | 
481 |     for (String inexistentName : allJsDocParams) {
482 |       reportWarning(INEXISTANT_PARAM, inexistentName, fnName);
483 |     }
484 | 
485 |     parametersNode = builder.build();
486 |     return this;
487 |   }

/**
   * Visits a VAR node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1381 |   private void visitVar(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1382 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Fix this so that the doc info always shows up
1383 |     // on the NAME node. We probably want to wait for the parser
1384 |     // merge to fix this.
1385 |     JSDocInfo varInfo = n.hasOneChild() ? n.getJSDocInfo() : null;
1386 |     for (Node name : n.children()) {
1387 |       Node value = name.getFirstChild();
1388 |       // A null var would indicate a bug in the scope creation logic.
1389 |       Var var = t.getScope().getVar(name.getString());
1390 | 
1391 |       if (value != null) {
1392 |         JSType valueType = getJSType(value);
1393 |         JSType nameType = var.getType();
1394 |         nameType = (nameType == null) ? getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE) : nameType;
1395 | 
1396 |         JSDocInfo info = name.getJSDocInfo();
1397 |         if (info == null) {
1398 |           info = varInfo;
1399 |         }
1400 | 
1401 |         checkEnumAlias(t, info, value);
1402 |         if (var.isTypeInferred()) {
1403 |           ensureTyped(t, name, valueType);
1404 |         } else {
1405 |           validator.expectCanAssignTo(
1406 |               t, value, valueType, nameType, "initializing variable");
1407 |         }
1408 |       }
1409 |     }
1410 |   }

===== M0742 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 124-178
Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
124 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
125 |     switch (n.getType()) {
126 |       case Token.VAR:
127 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
128 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
129 |              child != null;) {
130 |           Node next = child.getNext();
131 |           declareVar(child);
132 |           child = next;
133 |         }
134 |         return;
135 | 
136 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
137 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
138 |           return;
139 |         }
140 | 
141 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
142 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
143 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
144 |           return;
145 |         }
146 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
147 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
148 | 
149 |       case Token.CATCH:
150 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
151 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().isName());
152 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
153 |         // is the code block
154 | 
155 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
156 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
157 | 
158 |         declareVar(var);
159 |         scanVars(block, n);
160 |         return;  // only one child to scan
161 | 
162 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
163 |         inputId = n.getInputId();
164 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(inputId);
165 |         break;
166 |     }
167 | 
168 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
169 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
170 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
171 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
172 |            child != null;) {
173 |         Node next = child.getNext();
174 |         scanVars(child, n);
175 |         child = next;
176 |       }
177 |     }
178 |   }

Original range: 227-290
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
227 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
228 |     this.options = options;
229 |     if (errorManager == null) {
230 |       if (outStream == null) {
231 |         setErrorManager(
232 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
233 |       } else {
234 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
235 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
236 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
237 |         setErrorManager(printer);
238 |       }
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
242 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
243 |       options.checkTypes = true;
244 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
245 |       options.checkTypes = false;
246 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
247 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
248 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
249 |       // parser type warnings.
250 |       options.setWarningLevel(
251 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
252 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
253 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn() &&
257 |         !options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS)) {
258 |       options.setWarningLevel(
259 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
260 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
261 |     }
262 | 
263 |     if (options.getLanguageIn() == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT5_STRICT) {
264 |       options.setWarningLevel(
265 |           DiagnosticGroups.ES5_STRICT,
266 |           CheckLevel.ERROR);
267 |     }
268 | 
269 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
270 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
271 |     guards.add(
272 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
273 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
274 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
275 | 
276 |     ComposeWarningsGuard composedGuards = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
277 | 
278 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
279 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
280 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
281 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
282 |     // error off.
283 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
284 |         !composedGuards.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES)) {
285 |       composedGuards.addGuard(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
286 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
287 |     }
288 | 
289 |     this.warningsGuard = composedGuards;
290 |   }

/** Gets the minimum number of arguments that this function requires. */
Method code:
234 |   public int getMinArguments() {
235 |     // NOTE(nicksantos): There are some native functions that have optional
236 |     // parameters before required parameters. This algorithm finds the position
237 |     // of the last required parameter.
238 |     int i = 0;

/** Handle bleeding functions and function parameters. */
Method code:
1810 |     private void handleFunctionInputs(Node fnNode) {
1811 |       // Handle bleeding functions.
1812 |       Node fnNameNode = fnNode.getFirstChild();
1813 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
1814 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty()) {
1815 |         Scope.Var fnVar = scope.getVar(fnName);
1816 |         if (fnVar == null ||
1817 |             // Make sure we're not touching a native function. Native
1818 |             // functions aren't bleeding, but may not have a declaration
1819 |             // node.

1821 |                 // Make sure that the function is actually bleeding by checking
1822 |                 // if has already been declared.
1823 |                 fnVar.getInitialValue() != fnNode)) {
1824 |           defineSlot(fnNameNode, fnNode, fnNode.getJSType(), false);
1825 |         }
1826 |       }
1827 |

===== M0768 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowGraph.isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowGraph.java
Original range: 151-195
Documentation:
/**
   * @return True if n should be represented by a new CFG node in the control
   * flow graph.
   */
Method code:
151 |   public static boolean isEnteringNewCfgNode(Node n) {
152 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
153 |     switch (parent.getType()) {
154 |       case Token.BLOCK:
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |       case Token.TRY:
157 |         return true;
158 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
159 |         // A function node represents the start of a function where the name
160 |         // is bleed into the local scope and parameters has been assigned
161 |         // to the formal argument names. The node includes the name of the
162 |         // function and the LP list since we assume the whole set up process
163 |         // is atomic without change in control flow. The next change of
164 |         // control is going into the function's body represent by the second
165 |         // child.
166 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
167 |       case Token.WHILE:
168 |       case Token.DO:
169 |       case Token.IF:
170 |         // Theses control structure is represented by its node that holds the
171 |         // condition. Each of them is a branch node based on its condition.
172 |         return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
173 | 
174 |       case Token.FOR:
175 |         // The FOR(;;) node differs from other control structure in that
176 |         // it has a initialization and a increment statement. Those
177 |         // two statements have its corresponding CFG nodes to represent them.
178 |         // The FOR node represents the condition check for each iteration.
179 |         // That way the following:
180 |         // for(var x = 0; x < 10; x++) { } has a graph that is isomorphic to
181 |         // var x = 0; while(x<10) {  x++; }
182 |         if (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent)) {
183 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
184 |         } else {
185 |           return NodeUtil.getConditionExpression(parent) != n;
186 |         }
187 |       case Token.SWITCH:
188 |       case Token.CASE:
189 |       case Token.CATCH:
190 |       case Token.WITH:
191 |         return n != parent.getFirstChild();
192 |       default:
193 |         return false;
194 |     }
195 |   }

/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
470 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
471 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
472 |     int type = n.getType();
473 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
474 |       inputId = n.getInputId();
475 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
476 |     }
477 | 
478 |     curNode = n;
479 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
480 | 
481 |     switch (type) {
482 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
483 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
484 |         break;
485 | 
486 |       default:
487 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
488 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
489 |           // would no longer point to the true next
490 |           Node next = child.getNext();
491 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
492 |           child = next;
493 |         }
494 |         break;
495 |     }
496 | 
497 |     curNode = n;
498 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
499 |   }

/**
   * Infer the role of the function (whether it's a constructor or interface)
   * and what it inherits from in JSDocInfo.
   */
Method code:
316 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferInheritance(@Nullable JSDocInfo info) {
317 |     if (info != null) {
318 |       isConstructor = info.isConstructor();
319 |       isInterface = info.isInterface();
320 | 
321 |       // base type
322 |       if (info.hasBaseType()) {

325 |               info.getBaseType().evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
326 |           if (maybeBaseType != null &&
327 |               maybeBaseType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
328 |             baseType = (ObjectType) maybeBaseType;
329 |           }
330 |         } else {
331 |           reportWarning(EXTENDS_WITHOUT_TYPEDEF, fnName);
332 |         }
333 |       }
334 | 
335 |       // implemented interfaces
336 |       if (isConstructor || isInterface) {
337 |         implementedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
338 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
339 |           JSType maybeInterType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);
340 |           if (maybeInterType != null &&
341 |               maybeInterType.setValidator(new ImplementedTypeValidator())) {
342 |             implementedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterType);
343 |           }
344 |         }
345 |       } else if (info.getImplementedInterfaceCount() > 0) {
346 |         reportWarning(IMPLEMENTS_WITHOUT_CONSTRUCTOR, fnName);
347 |       }
348 | 
349 |       // extended interfaces (for interface only)
350 |       if (isInterface) {
351 |         extendedInterfaces = Lists.newArrayList();
352 |         for (JSTypeExpression t : info.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
353 |           JSType maybeInterfaceType = t.evaluate(scope, typeRegistry);

355 |               maybeInterfaceType.setValidator(new ExtendedTypeValidator())) {
356 |             extendedInterfaces.add((ObjectType) maybeInterfaceType);
357 |           }
358 |         }

360 |     }

363 |   }

===== M0805 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visitFunction(NodeTraversal,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 1475-1536
Documentation:
/**
   * Visits a {@link Token#FUNCTION} node.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   */
Method code:
1475 |   private void visitFunction(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
1476 |     FunctionType functionType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(n.getJSType());
1477 |     String functionPrivateName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
1478 |     if (functionType.isConstructor()) {
1479 |       FunctionType baseConstructor = functionType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1480 |       if (baseConstructor != null &&
1481 |           baseConstructor != getNativeType(OBJECT_FUNCTION_TYPE) &&
1482 |           (baseConstructor.isInterface() && functionType.isConstructor())) {
1483 |         compiler.report(
1484 |             t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1485 |       } else {
1486 |         // All interfaces are properly implemented by a class
1487 |         for (JSType baseInterface : functionType.getImplementedInterfaces()) {
1488 |           boolean badImplementedType = false;
1489 |           ObjectType baseInterfaceObj = ObjectType.cast(baseInterface);
1490 |           if (baseInterfaceObj != null) {
1491 |             FunctionType interfaceConstructor =
1492 |               baseInterfaceObj.getConstructor();
1493 |             if (interfaceConstructor != null &&
1494 |                 !interfaceConstructor.isInterface()) {
1495 |               badImplementedType = true;
1496 |             }
1497 |           } else {
1498 |             badImplementedType = true;
1499 |           }
1500 |           if (badImplementedType) {
1501 |             report(t, n, BAD_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName);
1502 |           }
1503 |         }
1504 |         // check properties
1505 |         validator.expectAllInterfaceProperties(t, n, functionType);
1506 |       }
1507 |     } else if (functionType.isInterface()) {
1508 |       // Interface must extend only interfaces
1509 |       for (ObjectType extInterface : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1510 |         if (extInterface.getConstructor() != null
1511 |             && !extInterface.getConstructor().isInterface()) {
1512 |           compiler.report(
1513 |               t.makeError(n, CONFLICTING_EXTENDED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1514 |         }
1515 |       }
1516 |       // Interface cannot implement any interfaces
1517 |       if (functionType.hasImplementedInterfaces()) {
1518 |         compiler.report(t.makeError(n,
1519 |             CONFLICTING_IMPLEMENTED_TYPE, functionPrivateName));
1520 |       }
1521 |       // Check whether the extended interfaces have any conflicts
1522 |       if (functionType.getExtendedInterfacesCount() > 1) {
1523 |         // Only check when extending more than one interfaces
1524 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> properties
1525 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1526 |         HashMap<String, ObjectType> currentProperties
1527 |             = new HashMap<String, ObjectType>();
1528 |         for (ObjectType interfaceType : functionType.getExtendedInterfaces()) {
1529 |           currentProperties.clear();
1530 |           checkInterfaceConflictProperties(t, n, functionPrivateName,
1531 |               properties, currentProperties, interfaceType);
1532 |           properties.putAll(currentProperties);
1533 |         }
1534 |       }
1535 |     }
1536 |   }

/**
   * Looks for a type expression at the current token and if found,
   * returns it. Note that this method consumes input.
   *
   * Parameter type expressions are special for two reasons:
   * <ol>
   *   <li>They must begin with '{', to distinguish type names from param names.
   *   <li>They may end in '=', to denote optionality.
   * </ol>
   *
   * @param token The current token.
   * @return The type expression found or null if none.
   */
Method code:
1104 |   private Node parseAndRecordParamTypeNode(JsDocToken token) {
1105 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1106 |     int lineno = stream.getLineno();
1107 |     int startCharno = stream.getCharno();
1108 | 
1109 |     Node typeNode = parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(token);
1110 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1111 |       int endLineno = stream.getLineno();
1112 |       int endCharno = stream.getCharno();
1113 | 
1114 |       jsdocBuilder.markTypeNode(typeNode, lineno, startCharno,
1115 |           endLineno, endCharno, true);
1116 |     }
1117 |     return typeNode;
1118 |   }

===== M0813 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.LinkedFlowScope.equals(Object)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/LinkedFlowScope.java
Original range: 256-306
Documentation:

Method code:
256 |   @Override
257 |   public boolean equals(Object other) {
258 |     if (other instanceof LinkedFlowScope) {
259 |       LinkedFlowScope that = (LinkedFlowScope) other;
260 |       if (this.optimize() == that.optimize()) {
261 |         return true;
262 |       }
263 | 
264 |       // If two flow scopes are in the same function, then they could have
265 |       // two possible function scopes: the real one and the BOTTOM scope.
266 |       // If they have different function scopes, we *should* iterate thru all
267 |       // the variables in each scope and compare. However, 99.9% of the time,
268 |       // they're not equal. And the other .1% of the time, we can pretend
269 |       // they're equal--this just means that data flow analysis will have
270 |       // to propagate the entry lattice a little bit further than it
271 |       // really needs to. Everything will still come out ok.
272 |       if (this.getFunctionScope() != that.getFunctionScope()) {
273 |         return false;
274 |       }
275 | 
276 |       if (cache == that.cache) {
277 |         // If the two flow scopes have the same cache, then we can check
278 |         // equality a lot faster: by just looking at the "dirty" elements
279 |         // in the cache, and comparing them in both scopes.
280 |         for (String name : cache.dirtySymbols) {
281 |           if (diffSlots(getSlot(name), that.getSlot(name))) {
282 |             return false;
283 |           }
284 |         }
285 | 
286 |         return true;
287 |       }
288 | 
289 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> myFlowSlots = allFlowSlots();
290 |       Map<String, StaticSlot<JSType>> otherFlowSlots = that.allFlowSlots();
291 | 
292 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : myFlowSlots.values()) {
293 |         if (diffSlots(slot, otherFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
294 |           return false;
295 |         }
296 |         otherFlowSlots.remove(slot.getName());
297 |       }
298 |       for (StaticSlot<JSType> slot : otherFlowSlots.values()) {
299 |         if (diffSlots(slot, myFlowSlots.get(slot.getName()))) {
300 |           return false;
301 |         }
302 |       }
303 |       return true;
304 |     }
305 |     return false;
306 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.inferFromOverriddenFunction(FunctionType,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 242-293
Documentation:
/**
   * Infer the parameter and return types of a function from
   * the parameter and return types of the function it is overriding.
   *
   * @param oldType The function being overridden. Does nothing if this is null.
   * @param paramsParent The LP node of the function that we're assigning to.
   *     If null, that just means we're not initializing this to a function
   *     literal.
   */
Method code:
242 |   FunctionTypeBuilder inferFromOverriddenFunction(
243 |       @Nullable FunctionType oldType, @Nullable Node paramsParent) {
244 |     if (oldType == null) {
245 |       return this;
246 |     }
247 | 
248 |     returnType = oldType.getReturnType();
249 |     returnTypeInferred = oldType.isReturnTypeInferred();
250 |     if (paramsParent == null) {
251 |       // Not a function literal.
252 |       parametersNode = oldType.getParametersNode();
253 |       if (parametersNode == null) {
254 |         parametersNode = new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry).build();
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       // We're overriding with a function literal. Apply type information
258 |       // to each parameter of the literal.
259 |       FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder =
260 |           new FunctionParamBuilder(typeRegistry);
261 |       Iterator<Node> oldParams = oldType.getParameters().iterator();
262 |       boolean warnedAboutArgList = false;
263 |       boolean oldParamsListHitOptArgs = false;
264 |       for (Node currentParam = paramsParent.getFirstChild();
265 |            currentParam != null; currentParam = currentParam.getNext()) {
266 |         if (oldParams.hasNext()) {
267 |           Node oldParam = oldParams.next();
268 |           Node newParam = paramBuilder.newParameterFromNode(oldParam);
269 | 
270 |           oldParamsListHitOptArgs = oldParamsListHitOptArgs ||
271 |               oldParam.isVarArgs() ||
272 |               oldParam.isOptionalArg();
273 | 
274 |           // The subclass method might right its var_args as individual
275 |           // arguments.
276 |           if (currentParam.getNext() != null && newParam.isVarArgs()) {
277 |             newParam.setVarArgs(false);
278 |             newParam.setOptionalArg(true);
279 |           }
280 |         } else {
281 |           warnedAboutArgList |= addParameter(
282 |               paramBuilder,
283 |               typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE),
284 |               warnedAboutArgList,
285 |               codingConvention.isOptionalParameter(currentParam) ||
286 |                   oldParamsListHitOptArgs,
287 |               codingConvention.isVarArgsParameter(currentParam));
288 |         }
289 |       }
290 |       parametersNode = paramBuilder.build();
291 |     }
292 |     return this;
293 |   }

===== M0831 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ClosureCodingConvention.getClassesDefinedByCall(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ClosureCodingConvention.java
Original range: 75-130
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>Understands several different inheritance patterns that occur in
   * Google code (various uses of {@code inherits} and {@code mixin}).
   */
Method code:
75 |   @Override
76 |   public SubclassRelationship getClassesDefinedByCall(Node callNode) {
77 |     Node callName = callNode.getFirstChild();
78 |     SubclassType type = typeofClassDefiningName(callName);
79 |     if (type != null) {
80 |       Node subclass = null;
81 |       Node superclass = callNode.getLastChild();
82 | 
83 |       // There are six possible syntaxes for a class-defining method:
84 |       // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
85 |       // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
86 |       // goog$inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
87 |       // SubClass.mixin(SuperClass.prototype)
88 |       // goog.mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
89 |       // goog$mixin(SubClass.prototype, SuperClass.prototype)
90 |       boolean isDeprecatedCall = callNode.getChildCount() == 2 &&
91 |           callName.isGetProp();
92 |       if (isDeprecatedCall) {
93 |         // SubClass.inherits(SuperClass)
94 |         subclass = callName.getFirstChild();
95 |       } else if (callNode.getChildCount() == 3) {
96 |         // goog.inherits(SubClass, SuperClass)
97 |         subclass = callName.getNext();
98 |       } else {
99 |         return null;
100 |       }
101 | 
102 |       if (type == SubclassType.MIXIN) {
103 |         // Only consider mixins that mix two prototypes as related to
104 |         // inheritance.
105 |         if (!endsWithPrototype(superclass)) {
106 |           return null;
107 |         }
108 |         if (!isDeprecatedCall) {
109 |           if (!endsWithPrototype(subclass)) {
110 |             return null;
111 |           }
112 |           // Strip off the prototype from the name.
113 |           subclass = subclass.getFirstChild();
114 |         }
115 |         superclass = superclass.getFirstChild();
116 |       }
117 | 
118 |       // bail out if either of the side of the "inherits"
119 |       // isn't a real class name. This prevents us from
120 |       // doing something weird in cases like:
121 |       // goog.inherits(MySubClass, cond ? SuperClass1 : BaseClass2)
122 |       if (subclass != null &&
123 |           subclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName() &&
124 |           superclass.isUnscopedQualifiedName()) {
125 |         return new SubclassRelationship(type, subclass, superclass);
126 |       }
127 |     }
128 | 
129 |     return null;
130 |   }

===== M0832 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.lookAheadForTypeAnnotation()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 2317-2333
Documentation:
/**
   * Look ahead for a type annotation by advancing the character stream.
   * Does not modify the token stream.
   * This is kind of a hack, and is only necessary because we use the token
   * stream to parse types, but need the underlying character stream to get
   * JsDoc descriptions.
   * @return Whether we found a type annotation.
   */
Method code:
2317 |   private boolean lookAheadForTypeAnnotation() {
2318 |     boolean matchedLc = false;
2319 |     int c;
2320 |     while (true) {
2321 |       c = stream.getChar();
2322 |       if (c == ' ') {
2323 |         continue;
2324 |       } else if (c == '{') {
2325 |         matchedLc = true;
2326 |         break;
2327 |       } else {
2328 |         break;
2329 |       }
2330 |     }
2331 |     stream.ungetChar(c);
2332 |     return matchedLc;
2333 |   }

===== M0838 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeCheck.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeCheck.java
Original range: 460-804
Documentation:
/**
   * This is the meat of the type checking.  It is basically one big switch,
   * with each case representing one type of parse tree node.  The individual
   * cases are usually pretty straightforward.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal object that supplies context, such as the
   * scope chain to use in name lookups as well as error reporting.
   * @param n The node being visited.
   * @param parent The parent of the node n.
   */
Method code:
460 |   @Override
461 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
462 |     JSType childType;
463 |     JSType leftType, rightType;
464 |     Node left, right;
465 |     // To be explicitly set to false if the node is not typeable.
466 |     boolean typeable = true;
467 | 
468 |     switch (n.getType()) {
469 |       case Token.NAME:
470 |         typeable = visitName(t, n, parent);
471 |         break;
472 | 
473 |       case Token.PARAM_LIST:
474 |         // If this is under a FUNCTION node, it is a parameter list and can be
475 |         // ignored here.
476 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.FUNCTION) {
477 |           ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getFirstChild()));
478 |         } else {
479 |           typeable = false;
480 |         }
481 |         break;
482 | 
483 |       case Token.COMMA:
484 |         ensureTyped(t, n, getJSType(n.getLastChild()));
485 |         break;
486 | 
487 |       case Token.TRUE:
488 |       case Token.FALSE:
489 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
490 |         break;
491 | 
492 |       case Token.THIS:
493 |         ensureTyped(t, n, t.getScope().getTypeOfThis());
494 |         break;
495 | 
496 |       case Token.NULL:
497 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NULL_TYPE);
498 |         break;
499 | 
500 |       case Token.NUMBER:
501 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
502 |         break;
503 | 
504 |       case Token.STRING:
505 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
506 |         if (!NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, n.getParent())) {
507 |           ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
508 |         } else {
509 |           // Object literal keys are not typeable
510 |           typeable = false;
511 |         }
512 |         break;
513 | 
514 |       case Token.GETTER_DEF:
515 |       case Token.SETTER_DEF:
516 |         // Object literal keys are handled with OBJECTLIT
517 |         break;
518 | 
519 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
520 |         ensureTyped(t, n, ARRAY_TYPE);
521 |         break;
522 | 
523 |       case Token.REGEXP:
524 |         ensureTyped(t, n, REGEXP_TYPE);
525 |         break;
526 | 
527 |       case Token.GETPROP:
528 |         visitGetProp(t, n, parent);
529 |         typeable = !(parent.isAssign() &&
530 |                      parent.getFirstChild() == n);
531 |         break;
532 | 
533 |       case Token.GETELEM:
534 |         visitGetElem(t, n);
535 |         // The type of GETELEM is always unknown, so no point counting that.
536 |         // If that unknown leaks elsewhere (say by an assignment to another
537 |         // variable), then it will be counted.
538 |         typeable = false;
539 |         break;
540 | 
541 |       case Token.VAR:
542 |         visitVar(t, n);
543 |         typeable = false;
544 |         break;
545 | 
546 |       case Token.NEW:
547 |         visitNew(t, n);
548 |         typeable = true;
549 |         break;
550 | 
551 |       case Token.CALL:
552 |         visitCall(t, n);
553 |         typeable = !NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent);
554 |         break;
555 | 
556 |       case Token.RETURN:
557 |         visitReturn(t, n);
558 |         typeable = false;
559 |         break;
560 | 
561 |       case Token.DEC:
562 |       case Token.INC:
563 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
564 |         validator.expectNumber(
565 |             t, left, getJSType(left), "increment/decrement");
566 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
567 |         break;
568 | 
569 |       case Token.NOT:
570 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
571 |         break;
572 | 
573 |       case Token.VOID:
574 |         ensureTyped(t, n, VOID_TYPE);
575 |         break;
576 | 
577 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
578 |         ensureTyped(t, n, STRING_TYPE);
579 |         break;
580 | 
581 |       case Token.BITNOT:
582 |         childType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
583 |         if (!childType.matchesInt32Context()) {
584 |           report(t, n, BIT_OPERATION, NodeUtil.opToStr(n.getType()),
585 |               childType.toString());
586 |         }
587 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
588 |         break;
589 | 
590 |       case Token.POS:
591 |       case Token.NEG:
592 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
593 |         validator.expectNumber(t, left, getJSType(left), "sign operator");
594 |         ensureTyped(t, n, NUMBER_TYPE);
595 |         break;
596 | 
597 |       case Token.EQ:
598 |       case Token.NE: {
599 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
600 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
601 | 
602 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
603 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
604 |         TernaryValue result =
605 |             leftTypeRestricted.testForEquality(rightTypeRestricted);
606 |         if (result != TernaryValue.UNKNOWN) {
607 |           if (n.getType() == Token.NE) {
608 |             result = result.not();
609 |           }
610 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST, leftType.toString(),
611 |               rightType.toString(), result.toString());
612 |         }
613 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
614 |         break;
615 |       }
616 | 
617 |       case Token.SHEQ:
618 |       case Token.SHNE: {
619 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
620 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
621 | 
622 |         JSType leftTypeRestricted = leftType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
623 |         JSType rightTypeRestricted = rightType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
624 |         if (!leftTypeRestricted.canTestForShallowEqualityWith(
625 |                 rightTypeRestricted)) {
626 |           report(t, n, DETERMINISTIC_TEST_NO_RESULT, leftType.toString(),
627 |               rightType.toString());
628 |         }
629 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
630 |         break;
631 |       }
632 | 
633 |       case Token.LT:
634 |       case Token.LE:
635 |       case Token.GT:
636 |       case Token.GE:
637 |         leftType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
638 |         rightType = getJSType(n.getLastChild());
639 |         if (rightType.isNumber()) {
640 |           validator.expectNumber(
641 |               t, n, leftType, "left side of numeric comparison");
642 |         } else if (leftType.isNumber()) {
643 |           validator.expectNumber(
644 |               t, n, rightType, "right side of numeric comparison");
645 |         } else if (leftType.matchesNumberContext() &&
646 |                    rightType.matchesNumberContext()) {
647 |           // OK.
648 |         } else {
649 |           // Whether the comparison is numeric will be determined at runtime
650 |           // each time the expression is evaluated. Regardless, both operands
651 |           // should match a string context.
652 |           String message = "left side of comparison";
653 |           validator.expectString(t, n, leftType, message);
654 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
655 |               t, n, leftType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
656 |           message = "right side of comparison";
657 |           validator.expectString(t, n, rightType, message);
658 |           validator.expectNotNullOrUndefined(
659 |               t, n, rightType, message, getNativeType(STRING_TYPE));
660 |         }
661 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
662 |         break;
663 | 
664 |       case Token.IN:
665 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
666 |         right = n.getLastChild();
667 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
668 |         rightType = getJSType(right);
669 |         validator.expectObject(t, n, rightType, "'in' requires an object");
670 |         validator.expectString(t, left, leftType, "left side of 'in'");
671 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
672 |         break;
673 | 
674 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
675 |         left = n.getFirstChild();
676 |         right = n.getLastChild();
677 |         leftType = getJSType(left);
678 |         rightType = getJSType(right).restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
679 | 
680 |         validator.expectAnyObject(
681 |             t, left, leftType, "deterministic instanceof yields false");
682 |         validator.expectActualObject(
683 |             t, right, rightType, "instanceof requires an object");
684 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
685 |         break;
686 | 
687 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
688 |         visitAssign(t, n);
689 |         typeable = false;
690 |         break;
691 | 
692 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
693 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
694 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
695 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
696 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
697 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
698 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
699 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
700 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
701 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
702 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
703 |       case Token.LSH:
704 |       case Token.RSH:
705 |       case Token.URSH:
706 |       case Token.DIV:
707 |       case Token.MOD:
708 |       case Token.BITOR:
709 |       case Token.BITXOR:
710 |       case Token.BITAND:
711 |       case Token.SUB:
712 |       case Token.ADD:
713 |       case Token.MUL:
714 |         visitBinaryOperator(n.getType(), t, n);
715 |         break;
716 | 
717 |       case Token.DELPROP:
718 |         ensureTyped(t, n, BOOLEAN_TYPE);
719 |         break;
720 | 
721 |       case Token.CASE:
722 |         JSType switchType = getJSType(parent.getFirstChild());
723 |         JSType caseType = getJSType(n.getFirstChild());
724 |         validator.expectSwitchMatchesCase(t, n, switchType, caseType);
725 |         typeable = false;
726 |         break;
727 | 
728 |       case Token.WITH: {
729 |         Node child = n.getFirstChild();
730 |         childType = getJSType(child);
731 |         validator.expectObject(
732 |             t, child, childType, "with requires an object");
733 |         typeable = false;
734 |         break;
735 |       }
736 | 
737 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
738 |         visitFunction(t, n);
739 |         break;
740 | 
741 |       // These nodes have no interesting type behavior.
742 |       case Token.LABEL:
743 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
744 |       case Token.SWITCH:
745 |       case Token.BREAK:
746 |       case Token.CATCH:
747 |       case Token.TRY:
748 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
749 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
750 |       case Token.BLOCK:
751 |       case Token.EMPTY:
752 |       case Token.DEFAULT_CASE:
753 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
754 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
755 |       case Token.THROW:
756 |         typeable = false;
757 |         break;
758 | 
759 |       // These nodes require data flow analysis.
760 |       case Token.DO:
761 |       case Token.FOR:
762 |       case Token.IF:
763 |       case Token.WHILE:
764 |         typeable = false;
765 |         break;
766 | 
767 |       // These nodes are typed during the type inference.
768 |       case Token.AND:
769 |       case Token.HOOK:
770 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
771 |       case Token.OR:
772 |         if (n.getJSType() != null) { // If we didn't run type inference.
773 |           ensureTyped(t, n);
774 |         } else {
775 |           // If this is an enum, then give that type to the objectlit as well.
776 |           if ((n.isObjectLit())
777 |               && (parent.getJSType() instanceof EnumType)) {
778 |             ensureTyped(t, n, parent.getJSType());
779 |           } else {
780 |             ensureTyped(t, n);
781 |           }
782 |         }
783 |         if (n.isObjectLit()) {
784 |           for (Node key : n.children()) {
785 |             visitObjLitKey(t, key, n);
786 |           }
787 |         }
788 |         break;
789 | 
790 |       default:
791 |         report(t, n, UNEXPECTED_TOKEN, Token.name(n.getType()));
792 |         ensureTyped(t, n);
793 |         break;
794 |     }
795 | 
796 |     // Don't count externs since the user's code may not even use that part.
797 |     typeable = typeable && !inExterns;
798 | 
799 |     if (typeable) {
800 |       doPercentTypedAccounting(t, n);
801 |     }
802 | 
803 |     checkNoTypeCheckSection(n, false);
804 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocTokenStream.getCharIgnoreLineEnd()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocTokenStream.java
Original range: 393-437
Documentation:

Method code:
393 |   private int getCharIgnoreLineEnd() {
394 |     if (ungetCursor != 0) {
395 |       cursor++;
396 |       --ungetCursor;
397 |       if (charno == -1) {
398 |         charno = getOffset();
399 |       }
400 |       return ungetBuffer[ungetCursor];
401 |     }
402 | 
403 |     for(;;) {
404 |       int c;
405 |       if (sourceCursor == sourceEnd) {
406 |         hitEOF = true;
407 |         if (charno == -1) {
408 |           charno = getOffset();
409 |         }
410 |         return EOF_CHAR;
411 |       }
412 |       cursor++;
413 |       c = sourceString.charAt(sourceCursor++);
414 | 
415 | 
416 |       if (c <= 127) {
417 |         if (c == '\n' || c == '\r') {
418 |           lineEndChar = c;
419 |           c = '\n';
420 |         }
421 |       } else {
422 |         if (isJSFormatChar(c)) {
423 |           continue;
424 |         }
425 |         if (ScriptRuntime.isJSLineTerminator(c)) {
426 |           lineEndChar = c;
427 |           c = '\n';
428 |         }
429 |       }
430 | 
431 |       if (charno == -1) {
432 |         charno = getOffset();
433 |       }
434 | 
435 |       return c;
436 |     }
437 |   }

Original range: 574-623
Documentation:
/**
   * Builds the function type, and puts it in the registry.
   */
Method code:
574 |   FunctionType buildAndRegister() {
575 |     if (returnType == null &&
576 |         !contents.mayHaveNonEmptyReturns() && !contents.mayBeFromExterns()) {
577 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
578 |       returnTypeInferred = true;
579 |     }
580 | 
581 |     if (returnType == null) {
582 |       returnType = typeRegistry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
583 |     }
584 | 
585 |     if (parametersNode == null) {
586 |       throw new IllegalStateException(
587 |           "All Function types must have params and a return type");
588 |     }
589 | 
590 |     FunctionType fnType;
591 |     if (isConstructor) {
592 |       fnType = getOrCreateConstructor();
593 |     } else if (isInterface) {
594 |       fnType = typeRegistry.createInterfaceType(
595 |           fnName, contents.getSourceNode());
596 |       if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
597 |         typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
598 |       }
599 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
600 |     } else {
601 |       fnType = new FunctionBuilder(typeRegistry)
602 |           .withName(fnName)
603 |           .withSourceNode(contents.getSourceNode())
604 |           .withParamsNode(parametersNode)
605 |           .withReturnType(returnType, returnTypeInferred)
606 |           .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
607 |           .withTemplateName(templateTypeName)
608 |           .build();
609 |       maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
610 |     }
611 | 
612 |     if (implementedInterfaces != null) {
613 |       fnType.setImplementedInterfaces(implementedInterfaces);
614 |     }
615 | 
616 |     if (extendedInterfaces != null) {
617 |       fnType.setExtendedInterfaces(extendedInterfaces);
618 |     }
619 | 
620 |     typeRegistry.clearTemplateTypeName();
621 | 
622 |     return fnType;
623 |   }

===== M0858 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 84-119
Documentation:

Method code:
84 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
85 |     if (n.isFunction()) {
86 |       if (inputId == null) {
87 |         inputId = NodeUtil.getInputId(n);
88 |         // TODO(johnlenz): inputId maybe null if the FUNCTION node is detached
89 |         // from the AST.
90 |         // Is it meaningful to build a scope for detached FUNCTION node?
91 |       }
92 | 
93 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
94 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
95 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
96 | 
97 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
98 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
99 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
100 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
101 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
102 |       }
103 | 
104 |       // Args: Declare function variables
105 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.isParamList());
106 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
107 |            a = a.getNext()) {
108 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.isName());
109 |         declareVar(a);
110 |       }
111 | 
112 |       // Body
113 |       scanVars(body, n);
114 |     } else {
115 |       // It's the global block
116 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
117 |       scanVars(n, null);
118 |     }
119 |   }

Method code:
280 |   private boolean isJSDocString(int c) {
281 |     switch (c) {
282 |       case '@':
283 |       case '*':
284 |       case ',':
285 |       case '>':
286 |       case ':':
287 |       case '(':
288 |       case ')':
289 |       case '{':
290 |       case '}':
291 |       case '[':
292 |       case ']':
293 |       case '?':
294 |       case '!':
295 |       case '|':
296 |       case '=':
297 |       case EOF_CHAR:
298 |       case '\n':
299 |         return false;
300 | 
301 |       default:
302 |         return !isJSSpace(c);
303 |     }
304 |   }

396 |   private void checkNoTypeCheckSection(Node n, boolean enterSection) {

===== M0877 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypeValidator.expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal,Node,JSType,JSType,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 343-360
Documentation:
/**
   * Expect that the first type can be assigned to a symbol of the second
   * type.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node to issue warnings on.
   * @param rightType The type on the RHS of the assign.
   * @param leftType The type of the symbol on the LHS of the assign.
   * @param owner The owner of the property being assigned to.
   * @param propName The name of the property being assigned to.
   * @return True if the types matched, false otherwise.
   */
Method code:
343 |   boolean expectCanAssignToPropertyOf(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType rightType,
344 |       JSType leftType, Node owner, String propName) {
345 |     // The NoType check is a hack to make typedefs work ok.
346 |     if (!leftType.isNoType() && !rightType.canAssignTo(leftType)) {
347 |       if (bothIntrinsics(rightType, leftType)) {
348 |         // We have a superior warning for this mistake, which gives you
349 |         // the line numbers of both types.
350 |         registerMismatch(rightType, leftType, null);
351 |       } else {
352 |         mismatch(t, n,
353 |             "assignment to property " + propName + " of " +
354 |             getReadableJSTypeName(owner, true),
355 |             rightType, leftType);
356 |       }
357 |       return false;
358 |     }
359 |     return true;
360 |   }

===== M0883 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parse()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 180-940
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses a {@link JSDocInfo} object. This parsing method reads all tokens
   * returned by the {@link JsDocTokenStream#getJsDocToken()} method until the
   * {@link JsDocToken#EOC} is returned.
   *
   * @return {@code true} if JSDoc information was correctly parsed,
   *     {@code false} otherwise
   */
Method code:
180 |   boolean parse() {
181 |     int lineno;
182 |     int charno;
183 | 
184 |     // JSTypes are represented as Rhino AST nodes, and then resolved later.
185 |     JSTypeExpression type;
186 | 
187 |     state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
188 |     skipEOLs();
189 | 
190 |     JsDocToken token = next();
191 | 
192 |     List<ExtendedTypeInfo> extendedTypes = Lists.newArrayList();
193 | 
194 |     // Always record that we have a comment.
195 |     if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
196 |       ExtractionInfo blockInfo = extractBlockComment(token);
197 |       token = blockInfo.token;
198 |       if (!blockInfo.string.isEmpty()) {
199 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription(blockInfo.string);
200 |       }
201 |     } else {
202 |       if (token != JsDocToken.ANNOTATION &&
203 |           token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
204 |         // Mark that there was a description, but don't bother marking
205 |         // what it was.
206 |         jsdocBuilder.recordBlockDescription("");
207 |       }
208 |     }
209 | 
210 |     // Parse the actual JsDoc.
211 |     retry: for (;;) {
212 |       switch (token) {
213 |         case ANNOTATION:
214 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
215 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
216 |             lineno = stream.getLineno();
217 |             charno = stream.getCharno();
218 | 
219 |             String annotationName = stream.getString();
220 |             Annotation annotation = annotationNames.get(annotationName);
221 |             if (annotation == null) {
222 |               parser.addParserWarning("msg.bad.jsdoc.tag", annotationName,
223 |                   stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
224 |             } else {
225 |               // Mark the beginning of the annotation.
226 |               jsdocBuilder.markAnnotation(annotationName, lineno, charno);
227 | 
228 |               switch (annotation) {
229 |                 case AUTHOR:
230 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
231 |                     ExtractionInfo authorInfo = extractSingleLineBlock();
232 |                     String author = authorInfo.string;
233 | 
234 |                     if (author.length() == 0) {
235 |                       parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.authormissing",
236 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
237 |                     } else {
238 |                       jsdocBuilder.addAuthor(author);
239 |                     }
240 |                     token = authorInfo.token;
241 |                   } else {
242 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
243 |                   }
244 |                   continue retry;
245 | 
246 |                 case CONSISTENTIDGENERATOR:
247 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConsistentIdGenerator()) {
248 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.consistidgen",
249 |                       stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
250 |                   }
251 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
252 |                   continue retry;
253 | 
254 |                 case CONSTANT:
255 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstancy()) {
256 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.const",
257 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
258 |                   }
259 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
260 |                   continue retry;
261 | 
262 |                 case CONSTRUCTOR:
263 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordConstructor()) {
264 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isInterfaceRecorded()) {
265 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
266 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
267 |                     } else {
268 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
269 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
270 |                     }
271 |                   }
272 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
273 |                   continue retry;
274 | 
275 |                 case DEPRECATED:
276 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecated()) {
277 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.deprecated",
278 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
279 |                   }
280 | 
281 |                   // Find the reason/description, if any.
282 |                   ExtractionInfo reasonInfo =
283 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
284 | 
285 |                   String reason = reasonInfo.string;
286 | 
287 |                   if (reason.length() > 0) {
288 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDeprecationReason(reason);
289 |                   }
290 | 
291 |                   token = reasonInfo.token;
292 |                   continue retry;
293 | 
294 |                 case INTERFACE:
295 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordInterface()) {
296 |                     if (jsdocBuilder.isConstructorRecorded()) {
297 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.interface.constructor",
298 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
299 |                     } else {
300 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.incompat.type",
301 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
302 |                     }
303 |                   }
304 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
305 |                   continue retry;
306 | 
307 |                 case DESC:
308 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.isDescriptionRecorded()) {
309 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.desc.extra",
310 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
311 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
312 |                     continue retry;
313 |                   } else {
314 |                     ExtractionInfo descriptionInfo =
315 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token);
316 | 
317 |                     String description = descriptionInfo.string;
318 | 
319 |                     jsdocBuilder.recordDescription(description);
320 |                     token = descriptionInfo.token;
321 |                     continue retry;
322 |                   }
323 | 
324 |                 case FILE_OVERVIEW:
325 |                   String fileOverview = "";
326 |                   if (jsdocBuilder.shouldParseDocumentation()) {
327 |                     ExtractionInfo fileOverviewInfo =
328 |                         extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
329 |                             WhitespaceOption.TRIM);
330 | 
331 |                     fileOverview = fileOverviewInfo.string;
332 | 
333 |                     token = fileOverviewInfo.token;
334 |                   } else {
335 |                     token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
336 |                   }
337 | 
338 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordFileOverview(fileOverview) ||
339 |                       fileOverviewJSDocInfo != null) {
340 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.fileoverview.extra",
341 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
342 |                   }
343 |                   continue retry;
344 | 
345 |                 case LICENSE:
346 |                 case PRESERVE:
347 |                   ExtractionInfo preserveInfo =
348 |                       extractMultilineTextualBlock(token,
349 |                                                    WhitespaceOption.PRESERVE);
350 | 
351 |                   String preserve = preserveInfo.string;
352 | 
353 |                   if (preserve.length() > 0) {
354 |                     if (fileLevelJsDocBuilder != null) {
355 |                       fileLevelJsDocBuilder.append(preserve);
356 |                     }
357 |                   }
358 | 
359 |                   token = preserveInfo.token;
360 |                   continue retry;
361 | 
362 |                 case ENUM:
363 |                   token = next();
364 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
365 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
366 | 
367 |                   type = null;
368 |                   if (token != JsDocToken.EOL && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
369 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(
370 |                         parseAndRecordTypeNode(token));
371 |                   }
372 | 
373 |                   if (type == null) {
374 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(newStringNode("number"));
375 |                   }
376 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordEnumParameterType(type)) {
377 |                     parser.addTypeWarning(
378 |                         "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
379 |                   }
380 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
381 |                   continue retry;
382 | 
383 |                 case EXPORT:
384 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExport()) {
385 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.export",
386 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
387 |                   }
388 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
389 |                   continue retry;
390 | 
391 |                 case EXTERNS:
392 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordExterns()) {
393 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.externs",
394 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
395 |                   }
396 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
397 |                   continue retry;
398 | 
399 |                 case JAVA_DISPATCH:
400 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordJavaDispatch()) {
401 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.javadispatch",
402 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
403 |                   }
404 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
405 |                   continue retry;
406 | 
407 |                 case EXTENDS:
408 |                 case IMPLEMENTS:
409 |                   skipEOLs();
410 |                   token = next();
411 |                   lineno = stream.getLineno();
412 |                   charno = stream.getCharno();
413 |                   boolean matchingRc = false;
414 | 
415 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.LC) {
416 |                     token = next();
417 |                     matchingRc = true;
418 |                   }
419 | 
420 |                   if (token == JsDocToken.STRING) {
421 |                     Node typeNode = parseAndRecordTypeNameNode(
422 |                         token, lineno, charno, matchingRc);
423 | 
424 |                     lineno = stream.getLineno();
425 |                     charno = stream.getCharno();
426 | 
427 |                     typeNode = wrapNode(Token.BANG, typeNode);
428 |                     if (typeNode != null && !matchingRc) {
429 |                       typeNode.putBooleanProp(Node.BRACELESS_TYPE, true);
430 |                     }
431 |                     type = createJSTypeExpression(typeNode);
432 | 
433 |                     if (annotation == Annotation.EXTENDS) {
434 |                       // record the extended type, check later
435 |                       extendedTypes.add(new ExtendedTypeInfo(
436 |                           type, stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno()));
437 |                     } else {
438 |                       Preconditions.checkState(
439 |                           annotation == Annotation.IMPLEMENTS);
440 |                       if (!jsdocBuilder.recordImplementedInterface(type)) {
441 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.implements.duplicate",
442 |                             lineno, charno);
443 |                       }
444 |                     }
445 |                     token = next();
446 |                     if (matchingRc) {
447 |                       if (token != JsDocToken.RC) {
448 |                         parser.addTypeWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc",
449 |                             stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
450 |                       }
451 |                     } else if (token != JsDocToken.EOL &&
452 |                         token != JsDocToken.EOF && token != JsDocToken.EOC) {
453 |                       parser.addTypeWarning("msg.end.annotation.expected",
454 |                           stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
455 |                     }
456 |                   } else {
457 |                     parser.addTypeWarning("msg.no.type.name", lineno, charno);
458 |                   }
459 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL(token);
460 |                   continue retry;
461 | 
462 |                 case HIDDEN:
463 |                   if (!jsdocBuilder.recordHiddenness()) {
464 |                     parser.addParserWarning("msg.jsdoc.hidden",
465 |                         stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());
466 |                   }
467 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
468 |                   continue retry;
469 | 
470 |                 case LENDS:
471 |                   skipEOLs();
472 | 
473 |                   matchingRc = false;
474 |                   if (match(JsDocToken.LC)) {
475 |                     token = next();
476 |                     matchingRc = true;
477 |                   }
478 |

878 |                         }

880 |

882 |                         if (!jsdocBuilder.recordType(type)) {
883 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
884 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
885 |                         }
886 |                         break;

890 |                           parser.addTypeWarning(
891 |                               "msg.jsdoc.incompat.type", lineno, charno);
892 |                         }
893 |                         break;

895 |                   }

897 |                   token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
898 |                   continue retry;
899 |               }
900 |             }
901 |           }
902 |           break;
903 | 
904 |         case EOC:
905 |           if (hasParsedFileOverviewDocInfo()) {
906 |             fileOverviewJSDocInfo = retrieveAndResetParsedJSDocInfo();
907 |           }
908 |           checkExtendedTypes(extendedTypes);
909 |           return true;
910 | 
911 |         case EOF:

913 |           jsdocBuilder.build(null);

915 |               stream.getLineno(), stream.getCharno());

919 |         case EOL:
920 |           if (state == State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE) {
921 |             state = State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION;
922 |           }
923 |           token = next();
924 |           continue retry;
925 | 
926 |         default:
927 |           if (token == JsDocToken.STAR && state == State.SEARCHING_ANNOTATION) {
928 |             token = next();

930 |           } else {
931 |             state = State.SEARCHING_NEWLINE;
932 |             token = eatTokensUntilEOL();
933 |             continue retry;
934 |           }
935 |       }
936 | 
937 |       // next token
938 |       token = next();
939 |     }
940 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.removeProp(PropListItem,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 771-784
Documentation:
/**
   * @param item The item to inspect
   * @param propType The property to look for
   * @return The replacement list if the property was removed, or
   *   'item' otherwise.
   */
Method code:
771 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {
772 |     if (item == null) {
773 |       return null;
774 |     } else if (item.getType() == propType) {
775 |       return item.getNext();
776 |     } else {
777 |       PropListItem result = removeProp(item.getNext(), propType);
778 |       if (result != item.getNext()) {
779 |         return item.chain(result);
780 |       } else {
781 |         return item;
782 |       }
783 |     }
784 |   }
