Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.AbstractScopeBuilder.defineSlot(Node,Node,String,JSType,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 1060-1193
Documentation:
/**
     * Defines a symbol in the current scope.
     *
     * @param n the defining NAME or GETPROP or object literal key node.
     * @param parent the {@code n}'s parent.
     * @param variableName The name that this should be known by.
     * @param type the variable's type. It may be {@code null} if
     *     {@code inferred} is {@code true}.
     * @param inferred Whether the type is inferred or declared.
     */
Method code:
1060 |     void defineSlot(Node n, Node parent, String variableName,
1061 |         JSType type, boolean inferred) {
1062 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(!variableName.isEmpty());
1063 | 
1064 |       boolean isGlobalVar = n.isName() && scope.isGlobal();
1065 |       boolean shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis =
1066 |           isGlobalVar &&
1067 |           (parent.isVar() ||
1068 |            parent.isFunction());
1069 | 
1070 |       // If n is a property, then we should really declare it in the
1071 |       // scope where the root object appears. This helps out people
1072 |       // who declare "global" names in an anonymous namespace.
1073 |       Scope scopeToDeclareIn = scope;
1074 |       if (n.isGetProp() && !scope.isGlobal() &&
1075 |           isQnameRootedInGlobalScope(n)) {
1076 |         Scope globalScope = scope.getGlobalScope();
1077 | 
1078 |         // don't try to declare in the global scope if there's
1079 |         // already a symbol there with this name.
1080 |         if (!globalScope.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1081 |           scopeToDeclareIn = scope.getGlobalScope();
1082 |         }
1083 |       }
1084 | 
1085 |       // declared in closest scope?
1086 |       CompilerInput input = compiler.getInput(inputId);
1087 |       if (scopeToDeclareIn.isDeclared(variableName, false)) {
1088 |         Var oldVar = scopeToDeclareIn.getVar(variableName);
1089 |         validator.expectUndeclaredVariable(
1090 |             sourceName, input, n, parent, oldVar, variableName, type);
1091 |       } else {
1092 |         if (!inferred) {
1093 |           setDeferredType(n, type);
1094 |         }
1095 | 
1096 |         // The input may be null if we are working with a AST snippet.
1097 |         boolean isExtern = n.isFromExterns();
1098 |         Var newVar =
1099 |             scopeToDeclareIn.declare(variableName, n, type, input, inferred);
1100 | 
1101 |         if (type instanceof EnumType) {
1102 |           Node initialValue = newVar.getInitialValue();
1103 |           boolean isValidValue = initialValue != null &&
1104 |               (initialValue.isObjectLit() ||
1105 |                initialValue.isQualifiedName());
1106 |           if (!isValidValue) {
1107 |             compiler.report(JSError.make(sourceName, n, ENUM_INITIALIZER));
1108 |           }
1109 |         }
1110 | 
1111 |         // We need to do some additional work for constructors and interfaces.
1112 |         FunctionType fnType = JSType.toMaybeFunctionType(type);
1113 |         if (fnType != null &&
1114 |             // We don't want to look at empty function types.
1115 |             !type.isEmptyType()) {
1116 |           if ((fnType.isConstructor() || fnType.isInterface()) &&
1117 |               !fnType.equals(getNativeType(U2U_CONSTRUCTOR_TYPE))) {
1118 |             // Declare var.prototype in the scope chain.
1119 |             FunctionType superClassCtor = fnType.getSuperClassConstructor();
1120 |             ObjectType.Property prototypeSlot = fnType.getSlot("prototype");
1121 | 
1122 |             String prototypeName = variableName + ".prototype";
1123 | 
1124 |             // There are some rare cases where the prototype will already
1125 |             // be declared. See TypedScopeCreatorTest#testBogusPrototypeInit.
1126 |             // Fortunately, other warnings will complain if this happens.
1127 |             if (scopeToDeclareIn.getOwnSlot(prototypeName) == null) {
1128 |               // When we declare the function prototype implicitly, we
1129 |               // want to make sure that the function and its prototype
1130 |               // are declared at the same node. We also want to make sure
1131 |               // that the if a symbol has both a Var and a JSType, they have
1132 |               // the same node.
1133 |               //
1134 |               // This consistency is helpful to users of SymbolTable,
1135 |               // because everything gets declared at the same place.
1136 |               prototypeSlot.setNode(n);
1137 | 
1138 |               scopeToDeclareIn.declare(prototypeName,
1139 |                   n, prototypeSlot.getType(), input,
1140 |                   /* declared iff there's an explicit supertype */
1141 |                   superClassCtor == null ||
1142 |                   superClassCtor.getInstanceType().equals(
1143 |                       getNativeType(OBJECT_TYPE)));
1144 |             }
1145 | 
1146 |             // Make sure the variable is initialized to something if
1147 |             // it constructs itself.
1148 |             if (newVar.getInitialValue() == null &&
1149 |                 !isExtern &&
1150 |                 // We want to make sure that when we declare a new instance
1151 |                 // type (with @constructor) that there's actually a ctor for it.
1152 |                 // This doesn't apply to structural constructors
1153 |                 // (like function(new:Array). Checking the constructed
1154 |                 // type against the variable name is a sufficient check for
1155 |                 // this.
1156 |                 variableName.equals(
1157 |                     fnType.getInstanceType().getReferenceName())) {
1158 |               compiler.report(
1159 |                   JSError.make(sourceName, n,
1160 |                       fnType.isConstructor() ?
1161 |                           CTOR_INITIALIZER : IFACE_INITIALIZER,
1162 |                       variableName));
1163 |             }
1164 |           }
1165 |         }
1166 |       }
1167 | 
1168 |       if (shouldDeclareOnGlobalThis) {
1169 |         ObjectType globalThis =
1170 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS);
1171 |         if (inferred) {
1172 |           globalThis.defineInferredProperty(variableName,
1173 |               type == null ?
1174 |               getNativeType(JSTypeNative.NO_TYPE) :
1175 |               type,
1176 |               n);
1177 |         } else {
1178 |           globalThis.defineDeclaredProperty(variableName, type, n);
1179 |         }
1180 |       }
1181 | 
1182 |       if (isGlobalVar && "Window".equals(variableName)
1183 |           && type != null
1184 |           && type.isFunctionType()
1185 |           && type.isConstructor()) {
1186 |         FunctionType globalThisCtor =
1187 |             typeRegistry.getNativeObjectType(GLOBAL_THIS).getConstructor();
1188 |         globalThisCtor.getInstanceType().clearCachedValues();
1189 |         globalThisCtor.getPrototype().clearCachedValues();
1190 |         globalThisCtor
1191 |             .setPrototypeBasedOn((type.toMaybeFunctionType()).getInstanceType());
1192 |       }
1193 |     }

===== M0897 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.TypedScopeCreator.createScope(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypedScopeCreator.java
Original range: 191-240
Documentation:
/**
   * Creates a scope with all types declared. Declares newly discovered types
   * and type properties in the type registry.
   */
Method code:
191 |   @Override
192 |   public Scope createScope(Node root, Scope parent) {
193 |     // Constructing the global scope is very different than constructing
194 |     // inner scopes, because only global scopes can contain named classes that
195 |     // show up in the type registry.
196 |     Scope newScope = null;
197 |     AbstractScopeBuilder scopeBuilder = null;
198 |     if (parent == null) {
199 |       // Run a first-order analysis over the syntax tree.
200 |       (new FirstOrderFunctionAnalyzer(compiler, functionAnalysisResults))
201 |           .process(root.getFirstChild(), root.getLastChild());
202 | 
203 |       // Find all the classes in the global scope.
204 |       newScope = createInitialScope(root);
205 | 
206 |       GlobalScopeBuilder globalScopeBuilder = new GlobalScopeBuilder(newScope);
207 |       scopeBuilder = globalScopeBuilder;
208 |       NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, scopeBuilder);
209 |     } else {
210 |       newScope = new Scope(parent, root);
211 |       LocalScopeBuilder localScopeBuilder = new LocalScopeBuilder(newScope);
212 |       scopeBuilder = localScopeBuilder;
213 |       localScopeBuilder.build();
214 |     }
215 | 
216 |     scopeBuilder.resolveStubDeclarations();
217 |     scopeBuilder.resolveTypes();
218 | 
219 |     // Gather the properties in each function that we found in the
220 |     // global scope, if that function has a @this type that we can
221 |     // build properties on.
222 |     for (Node functionNode : scopeBuilder.nonExternFunctions) {
223 |       JSType type = functionNode.getJSType();
224 |       if (type != null && type.isFunctionType()) {
225 |         FunctionType fnType = type.toMaybeFunctionType();
226 |         ObjectType fnThisType = fnType.getTypeOfThis();
227 |         if (!fnThisType.isUnknownType()) {
228 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, functionNode.getLastChild(),
229 |               scopeBuilder.new CollectProperties(fnThisType));
230 |         }
231 |       }
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     if (parent == null) {
235 |       codingConvention.defineDelegateProxyPrototypeProperties(
236 |           typeRegistry, newScope, delegateProxyPrototypes,
237 |           delegateCallingConventions);
238 |     }
239 |     return newScope;
240 |   }

===== M0902 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionTypeBuilder.getOrCreateConstructor()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 644-680
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns a constructor function either by returning it from the
   * registry if it exists or creating and registering a new type. If
   * there is already a type, then warn if the existing type is
   * different than the one we are creating, though still return the
   * existing function if possible.  The primary purpose of this is
   * that registering a constructor will fail for all built-in types
   * that are initialized in {@link JSTypeRegistry}.  We a) want to
   * make sure that the type information specified in the externs file
   * matches what is in the registry and b) annotate the externs with
   * the {@link JSType} from the registry so that there are not two
   * separate JSType objects for one type.
   */
Method code:
644 |   private FunctionType getOrCreateConstructor() {
645 |     FunctionType fnType = typeRegistry.createConstructorType(
646 |         fnName, contents.getSourceNode(), parametersNode, returnType);
647 |     JSType existingType = typeRegistry.getType(fnName);
648 | 
649 |     if (existingType != null) {
650 |       boolean isInstanceObject = existingType.isInstanceType();
651 |       if (isInstanceObject || fnName.equals("Function")) {
652 |         FunctionType existingFn =
653 |             isInstanceObject ?
654 |             existingType.toObjectType().getConstructor() :
655 |             typeRegistry.getNativeFunctionType(FUNCTION_FUNCTION_TYPE);
656 | 
657 |         if (existingFn.getSource() == null) {
658 |           existingFn.setSource(contents.getSourceNode());
659 |         }
660 | 
661 |         if (!existingFn.hasEqualCallType(fnType)) {
662 |           reportWarning(TYPE_REDEFINITION, fnName,
663 |               fnType.toString(), existingFn.toString());
664 |         }
665 | 
666 |         return existingFn;
667 |       } else {
668 |         // We fall through and return the created type, even though it will fail
669 |         // to register. We have no choice as we have to return a function. We
670 |         // issue an error elsewhere though, so the user should fix it.
671 |       }
672 |     }
673 | 
674 |     maybeSetBaseType(fnType);
675 | 
676 |     if (getScopeDeclaredIn().isGlobal() && !fnName.isEmpty()) {
677 |       typeRegistry.declareType(fnName, fnType.getInstanceType());
678 |     }
679 |     return fnType;
680 |   }

===== M0905 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InferJSDocInfo.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InferJSDocInfo.java
Original range: 92-189
Documentation:

Method code:
92 |   @Override
93 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
94 |     JSDocInfo docInfo;
95 | 
96 |     switch (n.getType()) {
97 |       // Infer JSDocInfo on types of all type declarations on variables.
98 |       case Token.NAME:
99 |         if (parent == null) {
100 |           return;
101 |         }
102 | 
103 |         // Only allow JSDoc on VARs, function declarations, and assigns.
104 |         if (parent.getType() != Token.VAR &&
105 |             !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(parent) &&
106 |             !(parent.isAssign() &&
107 |               n == parent.getFirstChild())) {
108 |           return;
109 |         }
110 | 
111 |         // There are four places the doc info could live.
112 |         // 1) A FUNCTION node.
113 |         // /** ... */ function f() { ... }
114 |         // 2) An ASSIGN parent.
115 |         // /** ... */ x = function () { ... }
116 |         // 3) A NAME parent.
117 |         // var x, /** ... */ y = function() { ... }
118 |         // 4) A VAR gramps.
119 |         // /** ... */ var x = function() { ... }
120 |         docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
121 |         if (docInfo == null &&
122 |             !(parent.isVar() &&
123 |                 !parent.hasOneChild())) {
124 |           docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
125 |         }
126 | 
127 |         // Try to find the type of the NAME.
128 |         JSType varType = n.getJSType();
129 |         if (varType == null && parent.isFunction()) {
130 |           varType = parent.getJSType();
131 |         }
132 | 
133 |         // If we have no type to attach JSDocInfo to, then there's nothing
134 |         // we can do.
135 |         if (varType == null || docInfo == null) {
136 |           return;
137 |         }
138 | 
139 |         // Dereference the type. If the result is not an object, or already
140 |         // has docs attached, then do nothing.
141 |         ObjectType objType = dereferenceToObject(varType);
142 |         if (objType == null || objType.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
143 |           return;
144 |         }
145 | 
146 |         attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(objType, docInfo, n.getString());
147 |         break;
148 | 
149 |       case Token.GETPROP:
150 |         // Infer JSDocInfo on properties.
151 |         // There are two ways to write doc comments on a property.
152 |         //
153 |         // 1)
154 |         // /** @deprecated */
155 |         // obj.prop = ...
156 |         //
157 |         // 2)
158 |         // /** @deprecated */
159 |         // obj.prop;
160 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent) ||
161 |             (parent.isAssign() &&
162 |              parent.getFirstChild() == n)) {
163 |           docInfo = n.getJSDocInfo();
164 |           if (docInfo == null) {
165 |             docInfo = parent.getJSDocInfo();
166 |           }
167 |           if (docInfo != null) {
168 |             ObjectType lhsType =
169 |                 dereferenceToObject(n.getFirstChild().getJSType());
170 |             if (lhsType != null) {
171 |               // Put the JSDoc in the property slot, if there is one.
172 |               String propName = n.getLastChild().getString();
173 |               if (lhsType.hasOwnProperty(propName)) {
174 |                 lhsType.setPropertyJSDocInfo(propName, docInfo);
175 |               }
176 | 
177 |               // Put the JSDoc in any constructors or function shapes as well.
178 |               ObjectType propType =
179 |                   dereferenceToObject(lhsType.getPropertyType(propName));
180 |               if (propType != null) {
181 |                 attachJSDocInfoToNominalTypeOrShape(
182 |                     propType, docInfo, n.getQualifiedName());
183 |               }
184 |             }
185 |           }
186 |         }
187 |         break;
188 |     }
189 |   }

/** @see #createFromTypeNodes(Node, String, StaticScope) */
Method code:
1452 |   private JSType createFromTypeNodesInternal(Node n, String sourceName,
1453 |       StaticScope<JSType> scope) {
1454 |     switch (n.getType()) {
1455 |       case Token.LC: // Record type.
1456 |         return createRecordTypeFromNodes(
1457 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1458 | 
1459 |       case Token.BANG: // Not nullable
1460 |         return createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1461 |             n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope)
1462 |             .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
1463 | 
1464 |       case Token.QMARK: // Nullable or unknown
1465 |         Node firstChild = n.getFirstChild();
1466 |         if (firstChild == null) {
1467 |           return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1468 |         }
1469 |         return createDefaultObjectUnion(
1470 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1471 |                 firstChild, sourceName, scope));
1472 | 
1473 |       case Token.EQUALS: // Optional
1474 |         return createOptionalType(
1475 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1476 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1477 | 
1478 |       case Token.ELLIPSIS: // Var args
1479 |         return createOptionalType(
1480 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1481 |                 n.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1482 | 
1483 |       case Token.STAR: // The AllType
1484 |         return getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
1485 | 
1486 |       case Token.LB: // Array type
1487 |         // TODO(nicksantos): Enforce membership restrictions on the Array.
1488 |         return getNativeType(ARRAY_TYPE);
1489 | 
1490 |       case Token.PIPE: // Union type
1491 |         UnionTypeBuilder builder = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
1492 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null;
1493 |              child = child.getNext()) {
1494 |           builder.addAlternate(
1495 |               createFromTypeNodesInternal(child, sourceName, scope));
1496 |         }
1497 |         return builder.build();
1498 | 
1499 |       case Token.EMPTY: // When the return value of a function is not specified
1500 |         return getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
1501 | 
1502 |       case Token.VOID: // Only allowed in the return value of a function.
1503 |         return getNativeType(VOID_TYPE);
1504 | 
1505 |       case Token.STRING:
1506 |         JSType namedType = getType(scope, n.getString(), sourceName,
1507 |             n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
1508 |         if (resolveMode != ResolveMode.LAZY_NAMES) {
1509 |           namedType = namedType.resolveInternal(reporter, scope);
1510 |         }
1511 |         if ((namedType instanceof ObjectType) &&
1512 |             !(nonNullableTypeNames.contains(n.getString()))) {
1513 |           Node typeList = n.getFirstChild();
1514 |           if (typeList != null &&
1515 |               ("Array".equals(n.getString()) ||
1516 |                "Object".equals(n.getString()))) {
1517 |             JSType parameterType =
1518 |                 createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1519 |                     typeList.getLastChild(), sourceName, scope);
1520 |             namedType = new ParameterizedType(
1521 |                 this, (ObjectType) namedType, parameterType);
1522 |             if (typeList.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1523 |               JSType indexType =
1524 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1525 |                       typeList.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope);
1526 |               namedType = new IndexedType(
1527 |                   this, (ObjectType) namedType, indexType);
1528 |             }
1529 |           }
1530 |           return createDefaultObjectUnion(namedType);
1531 |         } else {
1532 |           return namedType;
1533 |         }
1534 | 
1535 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
1536 |         ObjectType thisType = null;
1537 |         boolean isConstructor = false;
1538 |         Node current = n.getFirstChild();
1539 |         if (current.getType() == Token.THIS ||
1540 |             current.getType() == Token.NEW) {
1541 |           Node contextNode = current.getFirstChild();
1542 |           thisType =
1543 |               ObjectType.cast(
1544 |                   createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1545 |                       contextNode, sourceName, scope)
1546 |                   .restrictByNotNullOrUndefined());
1547 |           if (thisType == null) {
1548 |             reporter.warning(
1549 |                 ScriptRuntime.getMessage0(
1550 |                     current.getType() == Token.THIS ?
1551 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.thisnotobject" :
1552 |                     "msg.jsdoc.function.newnotobject"),
1553 |                 sourceName,
1554 |                 contextNode.getLineno(), contextNode.getCharno());
1555 |           }
1556 | 
1557 |           isConstructor = current.getType() == Token.NEW;
1558 |           current = current.getNext();
1559 |         }
1560 | 
1561 |         FunctionParamBuilder paramBuilder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1562 | 
1563 |         if (current.getType() == Token.PARAM_LIST) {
1564 |           Node args = current.getFirstChild();
1565 |           for (Node arg = current.getFirstChild(); arg != null;
1566 |                arg = arg.getNext()) {
1567 |             if (arg.getType() == Token.ELLIPSIS) {
1568 |               if (arg.getChildCount() == 0) {
1569 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE));
1570 |               } else {
1571 |                 paramBuilder.addVarArgs(
1572 |                     createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1573 |                         arg.getFirstChild(), sourceName, scope));
1574 |               }
1575 |             } else {
1576 |               JSType type = createFromTypeNodesInternal(
1577 |                   arg, sourceName, scope);
1578 |               if (arg.getType() == Token.EQUALS) {
1579 |                 boolean addSuccess = paramBuilder.addOptionalParams(type);
1580 |                 if (!addSuccess) {
1581 |                   reporter.warning(
1582 |                       ScriptRuntime.getMessage0("msg.jsdoc.function.varargs"),
1583 |                       sourceName, arg.getLineno(), arg.getCharno());
1584 |                 }
1585 |               } else {
1586 |                 paramBuilder.addRequiredParams(type);
1587 |               }
1588 |             }
1589 |           }
1590 |           current = current.getNext();
1591 |         }
1592 | 
1593 |         JSType returnType =
1594 |             createFromTypeNodesInternal(current, sourceName, scope);
1595 | 
1596 |         return new FunctionBuilder(this)
1597 |             .withParams(paramBuilder)
1598 |             .withReturnType(returnType)
1599 |             .withTypeOfThis(thisType)
1600 |             .setIsConstructor(isConstructor)
1601 |             .build();
1602 |     }
1603 | 
1604 |     throw new IllegalStateException(
1605 |         "Unexpected node in type expression: " + n.toString());
1606 |   }

/**
     * Annotate optional and var_arg function parameters.
     */
Method code:
223 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
224 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionJSDocInfo(n);
225 | 
226 |       // Compute which function parameters are optional and
227 |       // which are var_args.
228 |       Node args = n.getFirstChild().getNext();

===== M0928 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2024-2036
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether this node is used as an L-value. Notice that sometimes
   * names are used as both L-values and R-values.
   *
   * We treat "var x;" as a pseudo-L-value, which kind of makes sense if you
   * treat it as "assignment to 'undefined' at the top of the scope". But if
   * we're honest with ourselves, it doesn't make sense, and we only do this
   * because it makes sense to treat this as synactically similar to
   * "var x = 0;".
   *
   * @param n The node
   * @return True if n is an L-value.
   */
Method code:
2024 |   static boolean isLValue(Node n) {
2025 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(n.isName() || n.isGetProp() ||
2026 |         n.isGetElem());
2027 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
2028 |     return (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2029 |         || (NodeUtil.isForIn(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2030 |         || parent.isVar()
2031 |         || (parent.isFunction() && parent.getFirstChild() == n)
2032 |         || parent.isDec()
2033 |         || parent.isInc()
2034 |         || parent.isParamList()
2035 |         || parent.isCatch();
2036 |   }

/**

   *

   * for initialization is a subtype of the enum element type of
   * the enum the value is being copied in.</p>
   *

   * <pre>var myEnum = myOtherEnum;</pre>

===== M0960 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.ControlFlowAnalysis.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/ControlFlowAnalysis.java
Original range: 217-292
Documentation:

Method code:
217 |   @Override
218 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(
219 |       NodeTraversal nodeTraversal, Node n, Node parent) {
220 |     astPosition.put(n, astPositionCounter++);
221 | 
222 |     switch (n.getType()) {
223 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
224 |         if (shouldTraverseFunctions || n == cfg.getEntry().getValue()) {
225 |           exceptionHandler.push(n);
226 |           return true;
227 |         }
228 |         return false;
229 |       case Token.TRY:
230 |         exceptionHandler.push(n);
231 |         return true;
232 |     }
233 | 
234 |     /*
235 |      * We are going to stop the traversal depending on what the node's parent
236 |      * is.
237 |      *
238 |      * We are only interested in adding edges between nodes that change control
239 |      * flow. The most obvious ones are loops and IF-ELSE's. A statement
240 |      * transfers control to its next sibling.
241 |      *
242 |      * In case of an expression tree, there is no control flow within the tree
243 |      * even when there are short circuited operators and conditionals. When we
244 |      * are doing data flow analysis, we will simply synthesize lattices up the
245 |      * expression tree by finding the meet at each expression node.
246 |      *
247 |      * For example: within a Token.SWITCH, the expression in question does not
248 |      * change the control flow and need not to be considered.
249 |      */
250 |     if (parent != null) {
251 |       switch (parent.getType()) {
252 |         case Token.FOR:
253 |           // Only traverse the body of the for loop.
254 |           return n == parent.getLastChild();
255 | 
256 |         // Skip the conditions.
257 |         case Token.IF:
258 |         case Token.WHILE:
259 |         case Token.WITH:
260 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
261 |         case Token.DO:
262 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild().getNext();
263 |         // Only traverse the body of the cases
264 |         case Token.SWITCH:
265 |         case Token.CASE:
266 |         case Token.CATCH:
267 |         case Token.LABEL:
268 |           return n != parent.getFirstChild();
269 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
270 |           return n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext().getNext();
271 |         case Token.CONTINUE:
272 |         case Token.BREAK:
273 |         case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
274 |         case Token.VAR:
275 |         case Token.RETURN:
276 |         case Token.THROW:
277 |           return false;
278 |         case Token.TRY:
279 |           /* Just before we are about to visit the second child of the TRY node,
280 |            * we know that we will be visiting either the CATCH or the FINALLY.
281 |            * In other words, we know that the post order traversal of the TRY
282 |            * block has been finished, no more exceptions can be caught by the
283 |            * handler at this TRY block and should be taken out of the stack.
284 |            */
285 |           if (n == parent.getFirstChild().getNext()) {
286 |             Preconditions.checkState(exceptionHandler.peek() == parent);
287 |             exceptionHandler.pop();
288 |           }
289 |       }
290 |     }
291 |     return true;
292 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/TypeValidator.java
Original range: 477-485

/**
   * Expect that the first type can be cast to the second type. The first type
   * should be either a subtype or supertype of the second.
   *
   * @param t The node traversal.
   * @param n The node where warnings should point.
   * @param type The type being cast from.
   * @param castType The type being cast to.
   */
Method code:
477 |   void expectCanCast(NodeTraversal t, Node n, JSType type, JSType castType) {
478 |     castType = castType.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
479 |     type = type.restrictByNotNullOrUndefined();
480 | 
481 |     if (!type.canAssignTo(castType) && !castType.canAssignTo(type)) {
482 |       registerMismatch(type, castType, report(t.makeError(n, INVALID_CAST,
483 |           castType.toString(), type.toString())));
484 |     }
485 |   }

===== M0967 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.JsDocInfoParser.parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/JsDocInfoParser.java
Original range: 1544-1579
Documentation:
/**
   * ParamTypeExpressionAnnotation :=
   *     '{' OptionalParameterType '}' |
   *     '{' TopLevelTypeExpression '}' |
   *     '{' '...' TopLevelTypeExpression '}'
   *
   * OptionalParameterType :=
   *     TopLevelTypeExpression '='
   */
Method code:
1544 |   private Node parseParamTypeExpressionAnnotation(JsDocToken token) {
1545 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(token == JsDocToken.LC);
1546 | 
1547 |     skipEOLs();
1548 | 
1549 |     boolean restArg = false;
1550 |     token = next();
1551 |     if (token == JsDocToken.ELLIPSIS) {
1552 |       token = next();
1553 |       if (token == JsDocToken.RC) {
1554 |         // EMPTY represents the UNKNOWN type in the Type AST.
1555 |         return wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1556 |       }
1557 |       restArg = true;
1558 |     }
1559 | 
1560 |     Node typeNode = parseTopLevelTypeExpression(token);
1561 |     if (typeNode != null) {
1562 |       skipEOLs();
1563 |       if (restArg) {
1564 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.ELLIPSIS, typeNode);
1565 |       } else if (match(JsDocToken.EQUALS)) {
1566 |         next();
1567 |         skipEOLs();
1568 |         typeNode = wrapNode(Token.EQUALS, typeNode);
1569 |       }
1570 | 
1571 |       if (!match(JsDocToken.RC)) {
1572 |         reportTypeSyntaxWarning("msg.jsdoc.missing.rc");
1573 |       } else {
1574 |         next();
1575 |       }
1576 |     }
1577 | 
1578 |     return typeNode;
1579 |   }
